async function fetchWithTimeout(url,options={

}
,timeoutMs=1e4){

const id=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),timeoutMs);

...options,signal:controller.signal
}
);

}

async function generateContentWithRetryAndFallback(client,params,fallbackText){
const modelsToTry=[params.model,"gemini-3.1-flash-lite"];
let lastError=null;
for(const model of modelsToTry){
for(let attempt=1;
attempt<=2;
attempt++){
try{
console.log(`[Gemini Engine] Attempting model ${
model
}
 (Attempt ${
attempt
}
/2)...`);
const response=await client.models.generateContent({
...params,model
}
);
if(response
  && response.text){
console.log(`[Gemini Engine] Successfully generated content using model: ${
model
}
`);
return{
text:response.text,modelUsed:model
}

}

}
catch(err){
lastError=err;
console.warn(`[Gemini Engine Warning] Model ${
model
}
 failed on attempt ${
attempt
}
/2: ${
err.message
  || err
}
`);
if(attempt<2){
await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,500))
}

}

}

}
console.error("[Gemini Engine Error] All model attempts and retries exhausted. Applying heuristic fallback.");
return{
text:fallbackText,modelUsed:"fallback-heuristics"
}

}

const SYMBOLS=["EURUSD","GBPUSD","USDJPY","AUDUSD","BTCUSD","ETHUSD","SOLUSD","BNBUSD","AAPL","TSLA","MSFT","NVDA","XAUUSD","USOIL","XAGUSD","NGAS","SPX500","NDX100","DJI30","GER40"];
function normalizeSymbol(symStr){
if(!symStr)return null;
const upperSym=symStr.toUpperCase().trim();
for(const s of SYMBOLS){
if(s===upperSym)return s
}
const aliasMap={
"BTC":"BTCUSD","BITCOIN":"BTCUSD","ETH":"ETHUSD","ETHEREUM":"ETHUSD","SOL":"SOLUSD","SOLANA":"SOLUSD","BNB":"BNBUSD","EUR":"EURUSD","GBP":"GBPUSD","JPY":"USDJPY","AUD":"AUDUSD","APPLE":"AAPL","TESLA":"TSLA","MICROSOFT":"MSFT","NVIDIA":"NVDA","GOLD":"XAUUSD","XAU":"XAUUSD","OIL":"USOIL","WTI":"USOIL","SILVER":"XAGUSD","XAG":"XAGUSD","GAS":"NGAS","NASDAQ":"NDX100","DOW":"DJI30","DAX":"GER40"
}
;
for(const[key,val]of Object.entries(aliasMap)){
if(upperSym===key)return val
}
const sortedSymbols=[...SYMBOLS].sort((a,b)=>b.length-a.length);
for(const s of sortedSymbols){
if(upperSym.startsWith(s)){
return s
}

}
for(const s of sortedSymbols){
if(upperSym.includes(s)){
return s
}

}
return null
}
__name(normalizeSymbol,"normalizeSymbol");
function isPairEnabled(symbol){
if(!state
  || !state.config
  || !state.config.enabledPairs)return false;
const normalized=normalizeSymbol(symbol);
if(!normalized)return false;
return!!state.config.enabledPairs[normalized]
}
__name(isPairEnabled,"isPairEnabled");
function getSymbolMultipliers(symbol){
const norm=normalizeSymbol(symbol)
  || symbol
  || "";
const sym=norm.toUpperCase();
if(sym.includes("USD")
  && (sym.startsWith("EUR")
  || sym.startsWith("GBP")
  || sym.startsWith("AUD"))){
return{
pipMultiplier:1e4,dollarMultiplier:10
}

}
if(sym.includes("JPY")){
return{
pipMultiplier:100,dollarMultiplier:10
}

}
if(sym.startsWith("XAU")){
return{
pipMultiplier:100,dollarMultiplier:1
}

}
if(sym.startsWith("XAG")){
return{
pipMultiplier:100,dollarMultiplier:10
}

}
if(sym.startsWith("BTC")
  || sym.startsWith("ETH")
  || sym.startsWith("SOL")
  || sym.startsWith("BNB")){
return{
pipMultiplier:100,dollarMultiplier:.01
}

}
if(sym==="AAPL"
  || sym==="TSLA"
  || sym==="MSFT"
  || sym==="NVDA"){
return{
pipMultiplier:100,dollarMultiplier:1
}

}
return{
pipMultiplier:100,dollarMultiplier:1
}

}
__name(getSymbolMultipliers,"getSymbolMultipliers");
function calculateBaseLotSize(symbol,entryPrice,slPrice){
if(state
  && state.config
  && state.config.riskMode==="PERCENT"){
const capital=state.config.balance
  || 1e3;
const riskPercent=state.config.riskPerTrade
  || 1;
const riskAmount=capital*(riskPercent/100);
let slDistance=0;
if(slPrice
  && entryPrice){
slDistance=Math.abs(entryPrice-slPrice)
}
if(!slDistance
  || slDistance===0){
slDistance=entryPrice*.015
}
const{
pipMultiplier,dollarMultiplier
}
=getSymbolMultipliers(symbol);
const riskPerLot=slDistance*pipMultiplier*dollarMultiplier;
if(riskPerLot>0){
const calculatedLots=riskAmount/riskPerLot;
const leverage=state.config.leverage
  || 500;
return Math.max(.01,Math.min(50,Number(calculatedLots.toFixed(2))))
}

}
return Number(state?state.config?state.config.lotSize
  || .1:.1:.1)
}

function deepClone(obj){
return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(obj))

const generateHistory=__name((symbol,basePrice)=>{
const history=[];
let price=basePrice;
const now=new Date;
for(let i=60;
i>=0;
i--){
const time=new Date(now.getTime()-i*15*60*1e3).toISOString();
const drift=(Math.random()-.495)*(price*.003);
const open=price;
const close=price+drift;
const high=Math.max(open,close)+Math.random()*(price*.001);
const low=Math.min(open,close)-Math.random()*(price*.001);
price=close;
history.push({
time,open:Number(open.toFixed(symbol.includes("USD")
  && !symbol.startsWith("XAU")
  && !symbol.startsWith("BTC")?4:2)),high:Number(high.toFixed(symbol.includes("USD")
  && !symbol.startsWith("XAU")
  && !symbol.startsWith("BTC")?4:2)),low:Number(low.toFixed(symbol.includes("USD")
  && !symbol.startsWith("XAU")
  && !symbol.startsWith("BTC")?4:2)),close:Number(close.toFixed(symbol.includes("USD")
  && !symbol.startsWith("XAU")
  && !symbol.startsWith("BTC")?4:2)),volume:Math.floor(Math.random()*8e3+2e3)
}
)
}
computeIndicators(symbol,history);
return history
}
,"generateHistory");
function getHistoryForTimeframe(symbol,tf,currentPrice){
const asset=state.marketData[symbol];
if(!asset)return[];
if(tf==="M15"){
return asset.history
}
const history=[];
const now=new Date;
let intervalMs=15*60*1e3;
if(tf==="M1")intervalMs=1*60*1e3;
else if(tf==="M5")intervalMs=5*60*1e3;
else if(tf==="H1")intervalMs=60*60*1e3;
else if(tf==="H4")intervalMs=4*60*60*1e3;
else if(tf==="D1")intervalMs=24*60*60*1e3;
const seedString=`${
symbol
}
-${
tf
}
`;
let seed=0;
for(let i=0;
i<seedString.length;
i++){
seed+=seedString.charCodeAt(i)
}
function seededRandom(){
const x=Math.sin(seed++)*1e4;
return x-Math.floor(x)
}
__name(seededRandom,"seededRandom");
const decimals=symbol.includes("USD")
  && !symbol.startsWith("XAU")
  && !symbol.startsWith("BTC")?4:2;
let tempPrice=currentPrice;
const numBars=60;
for(let i=0;
i<numBars;
i++){
const time=new Date(now.getTime()-i*intervalMs).toISOString();
const vol=tf==="M1"?2e-4:tf==="M5"?5e-4:tf==="H1"?.002:tf==="H4"?.005:.012;
const waveFreq=tf==="M1"?12e4:tf==="M5"?3e5:tf==="H1"?6e5:tf==="H4"?12e5:24e5;
const wave=.08*Math.sin(Date.now()/waveFreq+i*.5);
const changePct=(seededRandom()-.495+wave)*vol;
const close=tempPrice;
const open=tempPrice/(1+changePct);
const high=Math.max(open,close)+seededRandom()*(tempPrice*vol*.2);
const low=Math.min(open,close)-seededRandom()*(tempPrice*vol*.2);
history.unshift({
time,open:Number(open.toFixed(decimals)),high:Number(high.toFixed(decimals)),low:Number(low.toFixed(decimals)),close:Number(close.toFixed(decimals)),volume:Math.floor(seededRandom()*8e3+2e3)
}
);
tempPrice=open
}
computeIndicators(symbol,history);
return history
}
__name(getHistoryForTimeframe,"getHistoryForTimeframe");
function computeIndicators(symbol,bars){
const fastPeriod=9;
const slowPeriod=21;
const rsiPeriod=14;
const atrPeriod=14;
const bbPeriod=20;
const k5=2/(5+1);
const k40=2/(40+1);
const k3=2/(3+1);
const k200=2/(200+1);
const k100=2/(100+1);
const k20=2/(20+1);
const k50=2/(50+1);
const k9=2/(9+1);
const k15=2/(15+1);
const k12=2/(12+1);
const k26=2/(26+1);
const kSignal=2/(9+1);
let lastEma5=bars[0].close;
let lastEma40=bars[0].close;
let lastEma200=bars[0].close;
let lastEma100=bars[0].close;
let lastEma20=bars[0].close;
let lastEma50=bars[0].close;
let lastEma9=bars[0].close;
let lastEma15=bars[0].close;
let lastEma12=bars[0].close;
let lastEma26=bars[0].close;
let lastEmaSignal=0;
const hybridRaws=[];
for(let i=0;
i<bars.length;
i++){
if(i>=fastPeriod){
let sum=0;
for(let j=0;
j<fastPeriod;
j++)sum+=bars[i-j].close;
bars[i].smaFast=Number((sum/fastPeriod).toFixed(4))
}
else{
bars[i].smaFast=bars[i].close
}
if(i>=slowPeriod){
let sum=0;
for(let j=0;
j<slowPeriod;
j++)sum+=bars[i-j].close;
bars[i].smaSlow=Number((sum/slowPeriod).toFixed(4))
}
else{
bars[i].smaSlow=bars[i].close
}
if(i>=rsiPeriod){
let gains=0;
let losses=0;
for(let j=0;
j<rsiPeriod;
j++){
const diff=bars[i-j].close-bars[i-j-1].close;
if(diff>0)gains+=diff;
else losses-=diff
}
const val=gains+losses===0?50:100*(gains/(gains+losses));
bars[i].rsi=Number(val.toFixed(1))
}
else{
bars[i].rsi=50+bars[i].close%15-7.5
}
let currentEma5=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma5=bars[i].close*k5+lastEma5*(1-k5)
}
bars[i].ema5=Number(currentEma5.toFixed(4));
lastEma5=currentEma5;
let currentEma40=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma40=bars[i].close*k40+lastEma40*(1-k40)
}
lastEma40=currentEma40;
let currentEma200=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma200=bars[i].close*k200+lastEma200*(1-k200)
}
bars[i].ema200=Number(currentEma200.toFixed(4));
lastEma200=currentEma200;
let currentEma20=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma20=bars[i].close*k20+lastEma20*(1-k20)
}
bars[i].ema20=Number(currentEma20.toFixed(4));
lastEma20=currentEma20;
let currentEma50=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma50=bars[i].close*k50+lastEma50*(1-k50)
}
bars[i].ema50=Number(currentEma50.toFixed(4));
lastEma50=currentEma50;
let currentEma100=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma100=bars[i].close*k100+lastEma100*(1-k100)
}
bars[i].ema100=Number(currentEma100.toFixed(4));
lastEma100=currentEma100;
let currentEma9=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma9=bars[i].close*k9+lastEma9*(1-k9)
}
bars[i].ema9=Number(currentEma9.toFixed(4));
lastEma9=currentEma9;
let currentEma15=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma15=bars[i].close*k15+lastEma15*(1-k15)
}
bars[i].ema15=Number(currentEma15.toFixed(4));
lastEma15=currentEma15;
let currentWma40=bars[i].close;
if(i>=39){
let sum=0;
let weightSum=0;
for(let j=0;
j<40;
j++){
const weight=40-j;
sum+=bars[i-j].close*weight;
weightSum+=weight
}
currentWma40=sum/weightSum
}
const hybridRaw=.7*currentEma40+.3*currentWma40;
hybridRaws.push(hybridRaw);
let currentEma12=bars[i].close;
let currentEma26=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma12=bars[i].close*k12+lastEma12*(1-k12);
currentEma26=bars[i].close*k26+lastEma26*(1-k26)
}
lastEma12=currentEma12;
lastEma26=currentEma26;
const macdLine=currentEma12-currentEma26;
let macdSig=macdLine;
if(i>0){
macdSig=macdLine*kSignal+lastEmaSignal*(1-kSignal)
}
lastEmaSignal=macdSig;
bars[i].macd=Number(macdLine.toFixed(5));
bars[i].macdSignal=Number(macdSig.toFixed(5));
bars[i].macdHist=Number((macdLine-macdSig).toFixed(5));
let tr=bars[i].high-bars[i].low;
if(i>0){
const prevClose=bars[i-1].close;
const tr1=bars[i].high-bars[i].low;
const tr2=Math.abs(bars[i].high-prevClose);
const tr3=Math.abs(bars[i].low-prevClose);
tr=Math.max(tr1,tr2,tr3)
}
if(i>=atrPeriod){
let trSum=0;
for(let j=0;
j<atrPeriod;
j++){
const idx=i-j;
let localTr=bars[idx].high-bars[idx].low;
if(idx>0){
const pc=bars[idx-1].close;
localTr=Math.max(bars[idx].high-bars[idx].low,Math.abs(bars[idx].high-pc),Math.abs(bars[idx].low-pc))
}
trSum+=localTr
}
bars[i].atr=Number((trSum/atrPeriod).toFixed(5))
}
else{
bars[i].atr=Number((bars[i].high-bars[i].low
  || bars[i].close*.0015).toFixed(5))
}
if(i>=bbPeriod){
let closeSum=0;
for(let j=0;
j<bbPeriod;
j++){
closeSum+=bars[i-j].close
}
const mean=closeSum/bbPeriod;
let varianceSum=0;
for(let j=0;
j<bbPeriod;
j++){
varianceSum+=Math.pow(bars[i-j].close-mean,2)
}
const variance=varianceSum/bbPeriod;
const stdDev=Math.sqrt(variance);
bars[i].bbMiddle=Number(mean.toFixed(4));
bars[i].bbUpper=Number((mean+2*stdDev).toFixed(4));
bars[i].bbLower=Number((mean-2*stdDev).toFixed(4))
}
else{
bars[i].bbMiddle=bars[i].close;
bars[i].bbUpper=Number((bars[i].close*1.01).toFixed(4));
bars[i].bbLower=Number((bars[i].close*.99).toFixed(4))
}

}
let lastActiveEma=hybridRaws[0];
for(let i=0;
i<bars.length;
i++){
let currentActiveEma=hybridRaws[i];
if(i>0){
currentActiveEma=hybridRaws[i]*k3+lastActiveEma*(1-k3)
}
bars[i].activeLine=Number(currentActiveEma.toFixed(4));
lastActiveEma=currentActiveEma
}
const medianPrices=bars.map(b=>(b.high+b.low)/2);
const smma8=[];
const smma5=[];
const smma3=[];
let sum8=0;
for(let i=0;
i<bars.length;
i++){
if(i<8){
sum8+=medianPrices[i];
if(i===7){
smma8.push(sum8/8)
}
else{
smma8.push(medianPrices[i])
}

}
else{
const prev=smma8[i-1];
smma8.push((prev*7+medianPrices[i])/8)
}

}
let sum5=0;
for(let i=0;
i<bars.length;
i++){
if(i<5){
sum5+=medianPrices[i];
if(i===4){
smma5.push(sum5/5)
}
else{
smma5.push(medianPrices[i])
}

}
else{
const prev=smma5[i-1];
smma5.push((prev*4+medianPrices[i])/5)
}

}
let sum3=0;
for(let i=0;
i<bars.length;
i++){
if(i<3){
sum3+=medianPrices[i];
if(i===2){
smma3.push(sum3/3)
}
else{
smma3.push(medianPrices[i])
}

}
else{
const prev=smma3[i-1];
smma3.push((prev*2+medianPrices[i])/3)
}

}
for(let i=0;
i<bars.length;
i++){
const jawIdx=i-5;
bars[i].alligatorJaw=jawIdx>=0?Number(smma8[jawIdx].toFixed(5)):Number(medianPrices[i].toFixed(5));
const teethIdx=i-3;
bars[i].alligatorTeeth=teethIdx>=0?Number(smma5[teethIdx].toFixed(5)):Number(medianPrices[i].toFixed(5));
const lipsIdx=i-2;
bars[i].alligatorLips=lipsIdx>=0?Number(smma3[lipsIdx].toFixed(5)):Number(medianPrices[i].toFixed(5))
}

}
__name(computeIndicators,"computeIndicators");

function generateStrongPasscode(){
const uppercase="ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ";
const lowercase="abcdefghijkmnopqrstuvwxyz";
const numbers="23456789";
const symbols="!@#%^*";
const all=uppercase+lowercase+numbers+symbols;
let passcode="QP-";
passcode+=uppercase[Math.floor(Math.random()*uppercase.length)];
passcode+=lowercase[Math.floor(Math.random()*lowercase.length)];
passcode+=numbers[Math.floor(Math.random()*numbers.length)];
passcode+=symbols[Math.floor(Math.random()*symbols.length)];
for(let i=0;
i<4;
i++){
passcode+=all[Math.floor(Math.random()*all.length)]
}
const chars=passcode.slice(3).split("");
for(let i=chars.length-1;
i>0;
i--){
const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));
[chars[i],chars[j]]=[chars[j],chars[i]]
}
return"QP-"+chars.join("")
}
__name(generateStrongPasscode,"generateStrongPasscode");

function sanitizeFirestore(obj){
if(obj===void 0){
return null
}
if(obj===null){
return null
}
if(obj instanceof Date){
return obj.toISOString()
}
if(typeof obj==="function"){
return null
}
if(Array.isArray(obj)){
return obj.map(sanitizeFirestore)
}
if(typeof obj==="object"){
const res={

}
;
for(const key of Object.keys(obj)){
const val=obj[key];
if(val!==void 0
  && typeof val!=="function"){
res[key]=sanitizeFirestore(val)
}
else{
res[key]=null
}

}
return res
}
return obj
}
__name(sanitizeFirestore,"sanitizeFirestore");
function withTimeout(promise,timeoutMs=15e3){
let timer;
const timeoutPromise=new Promise((_,reject)=>{
timer=setTimeout(()=>{
reject(new Error(`Firebase operation timed out (${
timeoutMs
}
ms limit) due to connection throttling or quota exhaustion.`))
}
,timeoutMs)
}
);
return Promise.race([promise,timeoutPromise]).finally(()=>clearTimeout(timer))
}
__name(withTimeout,"withTimeout");
let firebaseConsecutiveErrors=0;
let lastLogSweepTime=0;
let firestoreWriteTimeoutId=null;
let pendingTenantsToSave=new Set;
let isSavingToFirestore=false;
let demoLogWriteTimeoutId=null;
let pendingDemoLogsToSave=new Map;
let isSavingDemoLogs=false;

async function handleFirebaseError(err,context){
if(!firebaseEnabled)return;
const errorStr=String(err?.message
  || err?.code
  || err
  || "");
const isHardQuotaError=errorStr.includes("RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED")
  || errorStr.includes("quota")
  || errorStr.includes("Quota")
  || errorStr.includes("resource-exhausted")
  || errorStr.includes("limit exceeded")
  || errorStr.includes("exhausted")
  || errorStr.includes("timed out")
  || errorStr.includes("timeout")
  || errorStr.includes("Timeout")
  || err?.code==="resource-exhausted"
  || errorStr.includes("PERMISSION_DENIED")
  || errorStr.includes("permission-denied");
if(isHardQuotaError){
console.warn(`[Firebase Server] Expected quota limit / timeout / permissions issue during ${
context
}
: ${
errorStr
}
. Gracefully disabling Firestore and switching to local disk persistence.`);
await disableFirebaseGracefully();
return
}
firebaseConsecutiveErrors++;
console.warn(`[Firebase Server] Non-fatal Firebase warning #${
firebaseConsecutiveErrors
}
 during ${
context
}
: ${
errorStr
}
.`);
if(firebaseConsecutiveErrors>=10){
console.warn(`[Firebase Server] Too many consecutive transient connection timeouts/errors (${
firebaseConsecutiveErrors
}
). Gracefully disabling Firestore.`);
await disableFirebaseGracefully()
}

}
__name(handleFirebaseError,"handleFirebaseError");
async function disableFirebaseGracefully(){
if(firebaseEnabled){
console.warn("[Firebase Server] Firestore resource exhausted or connection issue. Gracefully falling back to high-performance disk-based persistence storage.");
firebaseEnabled=false;
const oldDb=db;
db=null;
if(oldDb){
try{
await terminate(oldDb);
console.log("[Firebase Server] Firestore connection terminated successfully to release active stream channels.")
}
catch(err){
console.warn("[Firebase Server] Non-blocking: Error while terminating Firestore client:",err)
}

}
if(isPrimary){
broadcastToWorkers()
}
else{
broadcastState()
}

}

}
__name(disableFirebaseGracefully,"disableFirebaseGracefully");
function getCollectionName(baseName){
return isProduction()?baseName:`${
baseName
}
_dev`
}

async function saveStateToFirestore(onlyTenantId,instant){
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db)return;
if(!isFirestoreInitialLoadComplete){
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Bypassing saveStateToFirestore. Initial load from Firestore is still in progress...`);
return
}
const targetId=onlyTenantId
  || activeTenantId;
if(targetId){
tenantLastLocalWrite[targetId]=Date.now();
tenantStateIsDirty[targetId]=true
}
else{
Object.keys(tenants).forEach(tId=>{
tenantLastLocalWrite[tId]=Date.now();
tenantStateIsDirty[tId]=true
}
)
}
if(instant){
if(!targetId
  || !tenants[targetId])return;
try{
const t=tenants[targetId];
const rawTrades=t.state.trades
  || [];
const openTrades=rawTrades.filter(x=>x.status==="OPEN");
const closedTrades=rawTrades.filter(x=>x.status!=="OPEN");
const trimmedTrades=[...openTrades,...closedTrades.slice(0,10)];
const trimmedSignals=(t.state.signals
  || []).slice(-10);
const trimmedAlerts=(t.state.telegramAlerts
  || []).slice(-5);
const trimmedRetests=(t.state.retestCandidates
  || []).slice(-5);
const tenantPayload={
name:t.name,email:t.email,username:t.username,password:t.password,passcode:t.passcode
  || t.password,tier:t.tier,status:t.status,price:t.price,nextBillingDate:t.nextBillingDate,limits:t.limits,disabledMenus:t.disabledMenus
  || [],stripeCustomerId:t.stripeCustomerId
  || "",stripeSubscriptionId:t.stripeSubscriptionId
  || "",stripeCurrentPeriodEnd:t.stripeCurrentPeriodEnd
  || 0,state:{
config:t.state.config,strategies:t.state.strategies,trades:trimmedTrades,signals:trimmedSignals,telegramAlerts:trimmedAlerts,news:t.state.news
  || [],mt5Config:t.state.mt5Config,aiNewsInsight:t.state.aiNewsInsight
  || "",retestCandidates:trimmedRetests
}
,id:targetId,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()
}
;
const tenantDocRef=doc(db,getCollectionName("tenants"),targetId);
await withTimeout(setDoc(tenantDocRef,sanitizeFirestore(tenantPayload)),30e3);
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Instant write successful for tenant: ${
targetId
}
`);
tenantStateIsDirty[targetId]=false;
tenantLastLocalWrite[targetId]=Date.parse(tenantPayload.updatedAt)
}
catch(err){
console.error(`[Firebase Server] Instant write failed for tenant: ${
targetId
}
:`,err)
}
return
}
if(targetId){
pendingTenantsToSave.add(targetId)
}
else{
Object.keys(tenants).forEach(tId=>pendingTenantsToSave.add(tId))
}
if(isSavingToFirestore){
return
}
if(firestoreWriteTimeoutId){
clearTimeout(firestoreWriteTimeoutId)
}
firestoreWriteTimeoutId=setTimeout(async()=>{
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db
  || isSavingToFirestore)return;
isSavingToFirestore=true;
const tenantsToSave=Array.from(pendingTenantsToSave);
pendingTenantsToSave.clear();
const filteredTenantsToSave=tenantsToSave.filter(tId=>{
if(!tenantStateIsDirty[tId]){
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Skipping debounced write for clean, idle tenant: "${
tId
}
"`);
return false
}
return true
}
);
if(filteredTenantsToSave.length===0){
console.log(`[Firebase Server] State sync skipped in background timeout: No tenants are dirty since the last sync.`);
isSavingToFirestore=false;
return
}
compactAndCleanState();
purgeExpiredFirestoreLogs().catch(e=>{
handleFirebaseError(e,"database sweep during logging").catch(()=>{

}
)
}
);
try{
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db)return;
const metadataDocRef=doc(db,getCollectionName("global_state"),"metadata");
await withTimeout(setDoc(metadataDocRef,sanitizeFirestore({
activeTenantId,pricingConfig,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()
}
)),30e3);
const promises=filteredTenantsToSave.map(async tId=>{
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db)return;
const t=tenants[tId];
if(!t)return;
try{
const tenantDocRef2=doc(db,getCollectionName("tenants"),tId);
const cloudSnap=await withTimeout(getDoc(tenantDocRef2),30e3);
if(cloudSnap.exists()){
const cloudData=cloudSnap.data();
const cloudUpdatedAt=cloudData.updatedAt?new Date(cloudData.updatedAt).getTime():0;
const localLastWrite=tenantLastLocalWrite[tId]
  || 0;
if(cloudData.state
  && cloudUpdatedAt>localLastWrite){
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Debounced save merged newer cloud config/strategies for tenant: ${
tId
}
`);
if(cloudData.state.config){
t.state.config={
...t.state.config,...cloudData.state.config
}

}
if(cloudData.state.strategies){
t.state.strategies=cloudData.state.strategies
}
tenantLastLocalWrite[tId]=cloudUpdatedAt
}

}

}
catch(err){
console.warn(`[Firebase Server] Non-blocking: Pre-save cloud merge failed for tenant: ${
tId
}
:`,err)
}
const rawTrades=t.state.trades
  || [];
const openTrades=rawTrades.filter(x=>x.status==="OPEN");
const closedTrades=rawTrades.filter(x=>x.status!=="OPEN");
const trimmedTrades=[...openTrades,...closedTrades.slice(0,10)];
const trimmedSignals=(t.state.signals
  || []).slice(-10);
const trimmedAlerts=(t.state.telegramAlerts
  || []).slice(-5);
const trimmedRetests=(t.state.retestCandidates
  || []).slice(-5);
const tenantPayload={
name:t.name,email:t.email,username:t.username,password:t.password,passcode:t.passcode
  || t.password,tier:t.tier,status:t.status,price:t.price,nextBillingDate:t.nextBillingDate,limits:t.limits,stripeCustomerId:t.stripeCustomerId
  || "",stripeSubscriptionId:t.stripeSubscriptionId
  || "",stripeCurrentPeriodEnd:t.stripeCurrentPeriodEnd
  || 0,state:{
config:t.state.config,strategies:t.state.strategies,trades:trimmedTrades,signals:trimmedSignals,telegramAlerts:trimmedAlerts,news:t.state.news
  || [],mt5Config:t.state.mt5Config,aiNewsInsight:t.state.aiNewsInsight
  || "",retestCandidates:trimmedRetests
}
,id:tId,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()
}
;
const tenantDocRef=doc(db,getCollectionName("tenants"),tId);
await withTimeout(setDoc(tenantDocRef,sanitizeFirestore(tenantPayload)),30e3);
tenantStateIsDirty[tId]=false;
tenantLastLocalWrite[tId]=Date.parse(tenantPayload.updatedAt)
}
);
await Promise.all(promises);
firebaseConsecutiveErrors=0;
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Successfully synced/debounced multi-tenant states to Firestore for tenants: ${
tenantsToSave.join(", ")
}
`)
}
catch(err){
tenantsToSave.forEach(tId=>pendingTenantsToSave.add(tId));
await handleFirebaseError(err,"saving state to Firestore")
}
finally{
isSavingToFirestore=false;
if(pendingTenantsToSave.size>0){
saveStateToFirestore()
}

}

}
,2500)
}
__name(saveStateToFirestore,"saveStateToFirestore");
async function saveDemoLogToFirestore(logData){
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db)return;
pendingDemoLogsToSave.set(logData.id,logData);
if(isSavingDemoLogs){
return
}
if(demoLogWriteTimeoutId){
clearTimeout(demoLogWriteTimeoutId)
}
demoLogWriteTimeoutId=setTimeout(async()=>{
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db
  || isSavingDemoLogs)return;
isSavingDemoLogs=true;
const logsToSave=Array.from(pendingDemoLogsToSave.values());
pendingDemoLogsToSave.clear();
try{
const promises=logsToSave.map(async log=>{
const logDocRef=doc(db,getCollectionName("demo_access_logs"),log.id);
await withTimeout(setDoc(logDocRef,sanitizeFirestore(log)),30e3)
}
);
await Promise.all(promises);
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Successfully synchronized ${
logsToSave.length
}
 throttled/debounced demo access logs directly to Firestore.`)
}
catch(err){
logsToSave.forEach(log=>pendingDemoLogsToSave.set(log.id,log));
await handleFirebaseError(err,"saving throttled demo access logs to Firestore")
}
finally{
isSavingDemoLogs=false
}

}
,3e4)
}
__name(saveDemoLogToFirestore,"saveDemoLogToFirestore");
function compactAndCleanState(){
console.log("[Compaction] Purging and compacting old, unused, temporary state histories in memory...");
try{
const today=new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
demoAccessLogs=(demoAccessLogs
  || []).filter(log=>log
  && (log.date===today
  || log.createdAt
  && log.createdAt.startsWith(today))).slice(-50);
Object.keys(tenants).forEach(tId=>{
const t=tenants[tId];
if(t
  && t.state){
if(t.state.config){
let balVal=Number(t.state.config.balance
  || 0);
if(balVal<1e3
  && balVal<999999999999){
const initialBalStr=balVal.toLocaleString(void 0,{
maximumFractionDigits:2
}
);
while(balVal<1e3){
balVal+=1e4
}
t.state.config.balance=balVal;
const openPnL=(t.state.trades
  || []).filter(tr=>tr
  && tr.status==="OPEN").reduce((sum,tr)=>sum+(tr.pnl
  || 0),0);
t.state.config.equity=Number((t.state.config.balance+openPnL).toFixed(2));
if(t.state.mt5Config){
if(!t.state.mt5Config.logs){
t.state.mt5Config.logs=[]
}
t.state.mt5Config.logs.unshift(`[Capital Assurance] Equity & Sizing safety guard triggered! Capital was $${
initialBalStr
}
, automatically added $10,000 top-ups. New Trading Capital: $${
balVal.toLocaleString(void 0,{
maximumFractionDigits:2
}
)
}
 USD.`)
}

}

}
const allTrades=t.state.trades
  || [];
const open=allTrades.filter(tr=>tr
  && tr.status==="OPEN");
const closed=allTrades.filter(tr=>tr
  && tr.status!=="OPEN");
closed.sort((a,b)=>{
const d1=new Date(b.closedAt
  || b.createdAt
  || 0).getTime();
const d2=new Date(a.closedAt
  || a.createdAt
  || 0).getTime();
return d1-d2
}
);
t.state.trades=[...open,...closed.slice(0,10)];
const signals=t.state.signals
  || [];
t.state.signals=signals.slice(-10);
const alerts=t.state.telegramAlerts
  || [];
t.state.telegramAlerts=alerts.slice(-5);
if(t.state.news){
t.state.news=ensureRollingNews(t.state.news);
t.state.news=t.state.news.slice(-20)
}
if(t.state.retestCandidates){
t.state.retestCandidates=t.state.retestCandidates.slice(-5)
}
if(t.state.mt5Config){
if(t.state.mt5Config.logs
  && Array.isArray(t.state.mt5Config.logs)){
t.state.mt5Config.logs=t.state.mt5Config.logs.slice(0,10)
}
if(t.state.mt5Config.mirrorActivity
  && Array.isArray(t.state.mt5Config.mirrorActivity)){
t.state.mt5Config.mirrorActivity=t.state.mt5Config.mirrorActivity.slice(0,10)
}

}
if(t.state.mlHistory
  && Array.isArray(t.state.mlHistory)){
t.state.mlHistory=t.state.mlHistory.slice(0,3)
}
if(t.state.mlRollbackPoints
  && Array.isArray(t.state.mlRollbackPoints)){
t.state.mlRollbackPoints=t.state.mlRollbackPoints.slice(0,3)
}
if(t.state.mlRecommendations
  && Array.isArray(t.state.mlRecommendations)){
t.state.mlRecommendations=t.state.mlRecommendations.slice(0,5)
}

}

}
);
console.log("[Compaction] In-memory multi-tenant state compacted successfully.")
}
catch(err){
console.error("[Compaction] Error during state compaction:",err)
}

}
__name(compactAndCleanState,"compactAndCleanState");
async function purgeExpiredFirestoreLogs(){
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db)return;
const now=Date.now();
if(now-lastLogSweepTime<6*60*60*1e3){
return
}
lastLogSweepTime=now;
try{
console.log("[Firebase Server] Initiating background Firestore log database sweep...");
const todayStr=new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
const logsCollectionRef=collection(db,getCollectionName("demo_access_logs"));
const snap=await withTimeout(getDocs(logsCollectionRef),30e3);
let deletedCount=0;
const deletePromises=[];
snap.forEach(docSnap=>{
const data=docSnap.data();
if(data
  && data.date!==todayStr){
const docRef=docSnap.ref;
deletePromises.push(deleteDoc(docRef).then(()=>{
deletedCount++
}
).catch(()=>{

}
))
}

}
);
if(deletePromises.length>0){
await Promise.all(deletePromises);
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Database Sweep Complete: Purged ${
deletedCount
}
 unused historical demo access log documents.`)
}
else{
console.log("[Firebase Server] Database Sweep: No expired audit logs found in Firestore.")
}

}
catch(err){
console.warn("[Firebase Server] Non-blocking database sweep encountered an exception:",err?.message
  || err);
await handleFirebaseError(err,"database sweep")
}

}
__name(purgeExpiredFirestoreLogs,"purgeExpiredFirestoreLogs");
async function loadDemoLogsFromFirestore(){
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db)return;
try{
const todayStr=new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
const logsCollectionRef=collection(db,getCollectionName("demo_access_logs"));
const logsQuery=query(logsCollectionRef,where("date","==",todayStr));
const snap=await withTimeout(getDocs(logsQuery),30e3);
const loaded=[];
snap.forEach(docSnap=>{
const data=docSnap.data();
if(data){
loaded.push(data)
}

}
);
demoAccessLogs=loaded;
firebaseConsecutiveErrors=0;
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Loaded ${
demoAccessLogs.length
}
 active demo access logs for today (${
todayStr
}
) from Firestore.`)
}
catch(err){
await handleFirebaseError(err,"fetching demo access logs")
}

}
__name(loadDemoLogsFromFirestore,"loadDemoLogsFromFirestore");
async function loadStateFromFirestore(onlyTenantId){
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db)return"disabled";
try{
if(!onlyTenantId){
const metadataDocRef=doc(db,getCollectionName("global_state"),"metadata");
const metaSnap=await withTimeout(getDoc(metadataDocRef),30e3);
if(metaSnap.exists()){
const metaData=metaSnap.data();
if(metaData.activeTenantId){
activeTenantId=metaData.activeTenantId
}
if(metaData.pricingConfig){
pricingConfig=metaData.pricingConfig
}

}

}
const processSavedTenant=__name((tId,savedT)=>{
if(!savedT)return;
const dbUpdatedAt=savedT.updatedAt?new Date(savedT.updatedAt).getTime():0;
const localLastModified=tenantLastLocalWrite[tId]
  || 0;
if(localLastModified>0
  && dbUpdatedAt<=localLastModified){
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Bypassing cloud state overwrite for tenant "${
tId
}
". Local memory state is equal to or newer than cloud data (local=${
new Date(localLastModified).toISOString()
}
 >= db=${
savedT.updatedAt
  || "none"
}
).`);
return
}
if(!tenants[tId]){
tenants[tId]={
id:tId,name:savedT.name
  || "Custom Operator",email:savedT.email
  || "operator@customdomain.com",username:savedT.username
  || tId.replace("tenant-",""),password:savedT.passcode
  || savedT.password
  || "@Hariom12",passcode:savedT.passcode
  || savedT.password
  || "@Hariom12",tier:savedT.tier
  || "Starter",status:savedT.status
  || "Active",price:savedT.price
  || 99,nextBillingDate:savedT.nextBillingDate
  || new Date(Date.now()+30*24*3600*1e3).toISOString().split("T")[0],limits:savedT.limits
  || {
maxStrategies:3,maxLotSize:.5,maxConcurrentTrades:3,aiSignalsAllowed:false
}
,stripeCustomerId:savedT.stripeCustomerId
  || "",stripeSubscriptionId:savedT.stripeSubscriptionId
  || "",stripeCurrentPeriodEnd:savedT.stripeCurrentPeriodEnd
  || 0,state:deepClone(state)
}

}
if(tenants[tId]){
tenants[tId].name=savedT.name
  || tenants[tId].name;
tenants[tId].email=savedT.email
  || tenants[tId].email;
tenants[tId].username=savedT.username
  || tenants[tId].username
  || tId.replace("tenant-","");
tenants[tId].password=savedT.passcode
  || savedT.password
  || tenants[tId].passcode
  || tenants[tId].password
  || "@Hariom12";
tenants[tId].passcode=tenants[tId].password;
tenants[tId].tier=savedT.tier
  || tenants[tId].tier;
tenants[tId].status=savedT.status
  || tenants[tId].status;
tenants[tId].price=savedT.price
  || tenants[tId].price;
tenants[tId].nextBillingDate=savedT.nextBillingDate
  || tenants[tId].nextBillingDate;
tenants[tId].limits=savedT.limits
  || tenants[tId].limits;
tenants[tId].disabledMenus=savedT.disabledMenus
  || [];
tenants[tId].stripeCustomerId=savedT.stripeCustomerId
  || "";
tenants[tId].stripeSubscriptionId=savedT.stripeSubscriptionId
  || "";
tenants[tId].stripeCurrentPeriodEnd=savedT.stripeCurrentPeriodEnd
  || 0;
if(savedT.state){
const tState=tenants[tId].state;
if(savedT.state.config)tState.config={
...tState.config,...savedT.state.config
}
;
if(savedT.state.strategies){
const incoming=savedT.state.strategies
  || [];
const merged=[...tState.strategies
  || []];
incoming.forEach(inS=>{
const idx=merged.findIndex(x=>x.id===inS.id);
if(idx!==-1){
merged[idx]={
...merged[idx],...inS
}

}
else{
merged.push(inS)
}

}
);
tState.strategies=merged
}
if(savedT.state.trades)tState.trades=savedT.state.trades;
if(savedT.state.signals)tState.signals=savedT.state.signals;
if(savedT.state.telegramAlerts)tState.telegramAlerts=savedT.state.telegramAlerts;
if(savedT.state.news)tState.news=savedT.state.news;
if(savedT.state.mt5Config)tState.mt5Config={
...tState.mt5Config,...savedT.state.mt5Config
}
;
if(savedT.state.aiNewsInsight)tState.aiNewsInsight=savedT.state.aiNewsInsight;
if(savedT.state.retestCandidates)tState.retestCandidates=savedT.state.retestCandidates;
migrateLoadedTenantState(tState)
}

}
tenantLastLocalWrite[tId]=dbUpdatedAt
}
,"processSavedTenant");
if(onlyTenantId){
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Direct load requested for selective occupant: "${
onlyTenantId
}
"`);
const tenantDocRef=doc(db,getCollectionName("tenants"),onlyTenantId);
const docSnap=await withTimeout(getDoc(tenantDocRef),30e3);
if(docSnap.exists()){
processSavedTenant(onlyTenantId,docSnap.data())
}

}
else{
const tenantsQuery=collection(db,getCollectionName("tenants"));
const snap=await withTimeout(getDocs(tenantsQuery),30e3);
if(snap.empty){
console.log("[Firebase Server] No tenants found in Firestore yet. Operating with local defaults.");
return"empty"
}
snap.forEach(docSnap=>{
processSavedTenant(docSnap.id,docSnap.data())
}
)
}
await loadDemoLogsFromFirestore();
firebaseConsecutiveErrors=0;
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Successfully restored state from Firestore (filter=${
onlyTenantId
  || "all"
}
).`);
return"success"
}
catch(err){
await handleFirebaseError(err,"loading state from Firestore");
return"error"
}

}

function saveState(){
compactAndCleanState();
try{
const backup={
activeTenantId,pricingConfig,tenants:{

}

}
;
Object.keys(tenants).forEach(tId=>{
const t=tenants[tId];
backup.tenants[tId]={
name:t.name,email:t.email,username:t.username,password:t.password,passcode:t.passcode
  || t.password,tier:t.tier,status:t.status,price:t.price,nextBillingDate:t.nextBillingDate,limits:t.limits,disabledMenus:t.disabledMenus
  || [],stripeCustomerId:t.stripeCustomerId
  || "",stripeSubscriptionId:t.stripeSubscriptionId
  || "",stripeCurrentPeriodEnd:t.stripeCurrentPeriodEnd
  || 0,state:{
config:t.state.config,strategies:t.state.strategies,trades:t.state.trades,signals:t.state.signals,telegramAlerts:t.state.telegramAlerts,news:t.state.news,mt5Config:t.state.mt5Config,aiNewsInsight:t.state.aiNewsInsight,retestCandidates:t.state.retestCandidates
}

}

}
);
fs.writeFileSync(STATE_FILE,JSON.stringify(backup,null,2),"utf-8");
if(isPrimary){
broadcastToWorkers()
}
else{
broadcastState()
}

}
catch(err){
console.error("[State Persistence] Failed to save multi-tenant state locally:",err)
}

}
__name(saveState,"saveState");
function broadcastToWorkers(){
if(isPrimary
  && cluster.workers){
const payload={
tenants,activeTenantId,pricingConfig,demoAccessLogs,latestLivePrices,firebaseEnabled
}
;
for(const id in cluster.workers){
const worker=cluster.workers[id];
if(worker
  && worker.isConnected()){
worker.send({
type:"SYNC_STATE",payload
}
)
}

}

}

}
__name(broadcastToWorkers,"broadcastToWorkers");

  && process.send){
process.send({
type:"MUTATION",payload:{
tenants,activeTenantId,pricingConfig,demoAccessLogs,latestLivePrices,firebaseEnabled
}

}
)
}

}

async function fetchBatchLivePrices(){
try{
const symbolMap={
"BTC-USD":"BTCUSD","ETH-USD":"ETHUSD","SOL-USD":"SOLUSD","BNB-USD":"BNBUSD","EURUSD=X":"EURUSD","GBPUSD=X":"GBPUSD","USDJPY=X":"USDJPY","AUDUSD=X":"AUDUSD","GC=F":"XAUUSD","CL=F":"USOIL","SI=F":"XAGUSD","NG=F":"NGAS","AAPL":"AAPL","TSLA":"TSLA","MSFT":"MSFT","NVDA":"NVDA","^GSPC":"SPX500","^NDX":"NDX100","^DJI":"DJI30","^GDAXI":"GER40"
}
;
const symbolsQuery=Object.keys(symbolMap).join(",");
const res=await fetchWithTimeout(`https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v7/finance/quote?symbols=${
symbolsQuery
}
`,{
headers:{
"User-Agent":"Mozilla/5.0"
}

}
);
if(!res.ok){
console.warn(`[Price Sync] Batch fetch response not OK: ${
res.status
}
`);
return
}
const data=await res.json();
const resultList=data?.quoteResponse?.result;
if(!Array.isArray(resultList))return;
resultList.forEach(item=>{
const liveSymbol=item.symbol;
const livePrice=item.regularMarketPrice;
const appSymbol=symbolMap[liveSymbol];
if(appSymbol
  && livePrice
  && !isNaN(livePrice)){
latestLivePrices[appSymbol]=livePrice;
BASE_PRICES[appSymbol]=livePrice
}

}
)
}
catch(err){
if(err
  && err.name==="AbortError"){
console.warn("[Price Sync] Batch live quote fetch timed out (Yahoo Finance unresponsive). Using simulated updates.")
}
else{
console.error("[Price Sync] Batch live quote fetch error:",err)
}

}

}
__name(fetchBatchLivePrices,"fetchBatchLivePrices");
if(isPrimary){
setInterval(()=>{
fetchBatchLivePrices()
}
,5e3);
fetchBatchLivePrices()
}

function pushTelegramAlert(alert){
const msgUpper=alert.message.toUpperCase();
const isBlockedOrPending=msgUpper.includes("BLOCKED")
  || msgUpper.includes("REJECTED")
  || msgUpper.includes("FAIL")
  || msgUpper.includes("CANCELLED")
  || msgUpper.includes("INVALID")
  || msgUpper.includes("IGNORED")
  || msgUpper.includes("SETUP DETECTED")
  || msgUpper.includes("CANDLE CLOSE CONFIRMED")
  || msgUpper.includes("SIGNAL BLOCKED")
  || msgUpper.includes("WAITING FOR RETEST")
  || msgUpper.includes("PRE-ENTRY")
  || msgUpper.includes("FIRST-ENTRY")
  || msgUpper.includes("BLOCKED ALERT")
  || msgUpper.includes("WAITING CONFIRMATION")
  || msgUpper.includes("WAITING")
  || msgUpper.includes("DIAGNOSTICS")
  || alert.type==="SYSTEM"
  && !msgUpper.includes("REVERSAL")
  && !msgUpper.includes("BILLING");
const isConfirmedExecution=msgUpper.includes("RETEST SETUP FILLED")
  || msgUpper.includes("NEW POSITION EXECUTED")
  || msgUpper.includes("OPERATOR MANUAL TRADING ENTRY")
  || msgUpper.includes("STOP-LOSS TRIGGERED")
  || msgUpper.includes("TAKE-PROFIT")
  || msgUpper.includes("EXIT ACHIEVED")
  || msgUpper.includes("POSITION CLOSE")
  || msgUpper.includes("TRAIL LOCKED")
  || msgUpper.includes("MANUAL CLOSE")
  || msgUpper.includes("REVERSAL TRIGGERED")
  || msgUpper.includes("TP1 HIT")
  || msgUpper.includes("TP2 HIT")
  || msgUpper.includes("TP_RATCHET")
  || msgUpper.includes("PARTIAL CLOSE")
  || msgUpper.includes("LOCK")
  || msgUpper.includes("BILLING");
if(isBlockedOrPending
  || !isConfirmedExecution){
console.log(`[Telegram Feed Filter] Suspended non-execution/blocked/pending alert: "${
alert.message.split("\n")[0]
}
"`);
return
}
state.telegramAlerts.unshift(alert);
const token=state.config.telegramReceiverToken;
const targetChatId=alert.chatId
  || state.config.telegramReceiverChatId;
if(state.config.telegramAlertsEnabled
  && token
  && token.includes(":")){
const botTokenStr=token.startsWith("bot")?token:"bot"+token;
fetchWithTimeout(`https://api.telegram.org/${
botTokenStr
}
/sendMessage`,{
method:"POST",headers:{
"Content-Type":"application/json"
}
,body:JSON.stringify({
chat_id:targetChatId,text:alert.message,parse_mode:"HTML"
}
)
}
,4e3).then(async res=>{
const data=await res.json();
if(res.ok
  && data.ok){
alert.deliveryStatus="SENT"
}
else{
alert.deliveryStatus="FAILED";
alert.errorLog=data.description
  || `HTTP ${
res.status
}
`
}

}
).catch(err=>{
alert.deliveryStatus="FAILED";
alert.errorLog=err.message
  || String(err)
}
)
}

}
__name(pushTelegramAlert,"pushTelegramAlert");

async function fetchWithTimeout(url,options={

}
,timeoutMs=1e4){
const controller=new AbortController;
const id=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),timeoutMs);
try{
const response=await fetch(url,{
...options,signal:controller.signal
}
);
clearTimeout(id);
return response
}
catch(error){
clearTimeout(id);
throw error
}

}
__name(fetchWithTimeout,"fetchWithTimeout");

async function generateContentWithRetryAndFallback(client,params,fallbackText){
const modelsToTry=[params.model,"gemini-3.1-flash-lite"];
let lastError=null;
for(const model of modelsToTry){
for(let attempt=1;
attempt<=2;
attempt++){
try{
console.log(`[Gemini Engine] Attempting model ${
model
}
 (Attempt ${
attempt
}
/2)...`);
const response=await client.models.generateContent({
...params,model
}
);
if(response
  && response.text){
console.log(`[Gemini Engine] Successfully generated content using model: ${
model
}
`);
return{
text:response.text,modelUsed:model
}

}

}
catch(err){
lastError=err;
console.warn(`[Gemini Engine Warning] Model ${
model
}
 failed on attempt ${
attempt
}
/2: ${
err.message
  || err
}
`);
if(attempt<2){
await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,500))
}

}

}

}
console.error("[Gemini Engine Error] All model attempts and retries exhausted. Applying heuristic fallback.");
return{
text:fallbackText,modelUsed:"fallback-heuristics"
}

}
__name(generateContentWithRetryAndFallback,"generateContentWithRetryAndFallback");
const SYMBOLS=["EURUSD","GBPUSD","USDJPY","AUDUSD","BTCUSD","ETHUSD","SOLUSD","BNBUSD","AAPL","TSLA","MSFT","NVDA","XAUUSD","USOIL","XAGUSD","NGAS","SPX500","NDX100","DJI30","GER40"];
function normalizeSymbol(symStr){
if(!symStr)return null;
const upperSym=symStr.toUpperCase().trim();
for(const s of SYMBOLS){
if(s===upperSym)return s
}
const aliasMap={
"BTC":"BTCUSD","BITCOIN":"BTCUSD","ETH":"ETHUSD","ETHEREUM":"ETHUSD","SOL":"SOLUSD","SOLANA":"SOLUSD","BNB":"BNBUSD","EUR":"EURUSD","GBP":"GBPUSD","JPY":"USDJPY","AUD":"AUDUSD","APPLE":"AAPL","TESLA":"TSLA","MICROSOFT":"MSFT","NVIDIA":"NVDA","GOLD":"XAUUSD","XAU":"XAUUSD","OIL":"USOIL","WTI":"USOIL","SILVER":"XAGUSD","XAG":"XAGUSD","GAS":"NGAS","NASDAQ":"NDX100","DOW":"DJI30","DAX":"GER40"
}
;
for(const[key,val]of Object.entries(aliasMap)){
if(upperSym===key)return val
}
const sortedSymbols=[...SYMBOLS].sort((a,b)=>b.length-a.length);
for(const s of sortedSymbols){
if(upperSym.startsWith(s)){
return s
}

}
for(const s of sortedSymbols){
if(upperSym.includes(s)){
return s
}

}
return null
}
__name(normalizeSymbol,"normalizeSymbol");
function isPairEnabled(symbol){
if(!state
  || !state.config
  || !state.config.enabledPairs)return false;
const normalized=normalizeSymbol(symbol);
if(!normalized)return false;
return!!state.config.enabledPairs[normalized]
}
__name(isPairEnabled,"isPairEnabled");
function getSymbolMultipliers(symbol){
const norm=normalizeSymbol(symbol)
  || symbol
  || "";
const sym=norm.toUpperCase();
if(sym.includes("USD")
  && (sym.startsWith("EUR")
  || sym.startsWith("GBP")
  || sym.startsWith("AUD"))){
return{
pipMultiplier:1e4,dollarMultiplier:10
}

}
if(sym.includes("JPY")){
return{
pipMultiplier:100,dollarMultiplier:10
}

}
if(sym.startsWith("XAU")){
return{
pipMultiplier:100,dollarMultiplier:1
}

}
if(sym.startsWith("XAG")){
return{
pipMultiplier:100,dollarMultiplier:10
}

}
if(sym.startsWith("BTC")
  || sym.startsWith("ETH")
  || sym.startsWith("SOL")
  || sym.startsWith("BNB")){
return{
pipMultiplier:100,dollarMultiplier:.01
}

}
if(sym==="AAPL"
  || sym==="TSLA"
  || sym==="MSFT"
  || sym==="NVDA"){
return{
pipMultiplier:100,dollarMultiplier:1
}

}
return{
pipMultiplier:100,dollarMultiplier:1
}

}
__name(getSymbolMultipliers,"getSymbolMultipliers");
function calculateBaseLotSize(symbol,entryPrice,slPrice){
if(state
  && state.config
  && state.config.riskMode==="PERCENT"){
const capital=state.config.balance
  || 1e3;
const riskPercent=state.config.riskPerTrade
  || 1;
const riskAmount=capital*(riskPercent/100);
let slDistance=0;
if(slPrice
  && entryPrice){
slDistance=Math.abs(entryPrice-slPrice)
}
if(!slDistance
  || slDistance===0){
slDistance=entryPrice*.015
}
const{
pipMultiplier,dollarMultiplier
}
=getSymbolMultipliers(symbol);
const riskPerLot=slDistance*pipMultiplier*dollarMultiplier;
if(riskPerLot>0){
const calculatedLots=riskAmount/riskPerLot;
const leverage=state.config.leverage
  || 500;
return Math.max(.01,Math.min(50,Number(calculatedLots.toFixed(2))))
}

}
return Number(state?state.config?state.config.lotSize
  || .1:.1:.1)
}
__name(calculateBaseLotSize,"calculateBaseLotSize");

}
;
return map[sym]
  || sym
}

function deepClone(obj){
return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(obj))
}

const generateHistory=__name((symbol,basePrice)=>{
const history=[];
let price=basePrice;
const now=new Date;
for(let i=60;
i>=0;
i--){
const time=new Date(now.getTime()-i*15*60*1e3).toISOString();
const drift=(Math.random()-.495)*(price*.003);
const open=price;
const close=price+drift;
const high=Math.max(open,close)+Math.random()*(price*.001);
const low=Math.min(open,close)-Math.random()*(price*.001);
price=close;
history.push({
time,open:Number(open.toFixed(symbol.includes("USD")
  && !symbol.startsWith("XAU")
  && !symbol.startsWith("BTC")?4:2)),high:Number(high.toFixed(symbol.includes("USD")
  && !symbol.startsWith("XAU")
  && !symbol.startsWith("BTC")?4:2)),low:Number(low.toFixed(symbol.includes("USD")
  && !symbol.startsWith("XAU")
  && !symbol.startsWith("BTC")?4:2)),close:Number(close.toFixed(symbol.includes("USD")
  && !symbol.startsWith("XAU")
  && !symbol.startsWith("BTC")?4:2)),volume:Math.floor(Math.random()*8e3+2e3)
}
)
}
computeIndicators(symbol,history);
return history
}

function getHistoryForTimeframe(symbol,tf,currentPrice){
const asset=state.marketData[symbol];
if(!asset)return[];
if(tf==="M15"){
return asset.history
}
const history=[];
const now=new Date;
let intervalMs=15*60*1e3;
if(tf==="M1")intervalMs=1*60*1e3;
else if(tf==="M5")intervalMs=5*60*1e3;
else if(tf==="H1")intervalMs=60*60*1e3;
else if(tf==="H4")intervalMs=4*60*60*1e3;
else if(tf==="D1")intervalMs=24*60*60*1e3;
const seedString=`${
symbol
}
-${
tf
}
`;
let seed=0;
for(let i=0;
i<seedString.length;
i++){
seed+=seedString.charCodeAt(i)
}
function seededRandom(){
const x=Math.sin(seed++)*1e4;
return x-Math.floor(x)
}
__name(seededRandom,"seededRandom");
const decimals=symbol.includes("USD")
  && !symbol.startsWith("XAU")
  && !symbol.startsWith("BTC")?4:2;
let tempPrice=currentPrice;
const numBars=60;
for(let i=0;
i<numBars;
i++){
const time=new Date(now.getTime()-i*intervalMs).toISOString();
const vol=tf==="M1"?2e-4:tf==="M5"?5e-4:tf==="H1"?.002:tf==="H4"?.005:.012;
const waveFreq=tf==="M1"?12e4:tf==="M5"?3e5:tf==="H1"?6e5:tf==="H4"?12e5:24e5;
const wave=.08*Math.sin(Date.now()/waveFreq+i*.5);
const changePct=(seededRandom()-.495+wave)*vol;
const close=tempPrice;
const open=tempPrice/(1+changePct);
const high=Math.max(open,close)+seededRandom()*(tempPrice*vol*.2);
const low=Math.min(open,close)-seededRandom()*(tempPrice*vol*.2);
history.unshift({
time,open:Number(open.toFixed(decimals)),high:Number(high.toFixed(decimals)),low:Number(low.toFixed(decimals)),close:Number(close.toFixed(decimals)),volume:Math.floor(seededRandom()*8e3+2e3)
}
);
tempPrice=open
}
computeIndicators(symbol,history);
return history
}
__name(getHistoryForTimeframe,"getHistoryForTimeframe");
function computeIndicators(symbol,bars){
const fastPeriod=9;
const slowPeriod=21;
const rsiPeriod=14;
const atrPeriod=14;
const bbPeriod=20;
const k5=2/(5+1);
const k40=2/(40+1);
const k3=2/(3+1);
const k200=2/(200+1);
const k100=2/(100+1);
const k20=2/(20+1);
const k50=2/(50+1);
const k9=2/(9+1);
const k15=2/(15+1);
const k12=2/(12+1);
const k26=2/(26+1);
const kSignal=2/(9+1);
let lastEma5=bars[0].close;
let lastEma40=bars[0].close;
let lastEma200=bars[0].close;
let lastEma100=bars[0].close;
let lastEma20=bars[0].close;
let lastEma50=bars[0].close;
let lastEma9=bars[0].close;
let lastEma15=bars[0].close;
let lastEma12=bars[0].close;
let lastEma26=bars[0].close;
let lastEmaSignal=0;
const hybridRaws=[];
for(let i=0;
i<bars.length;
i++){
if(i>=fastPeriod){
let sum=0;
for(let j=0;
j<fastPeriod;
j++)sum+=bars[i-j].close;
bars[i].smaFast=Number((sum/fastPeriod).toFixed(4))
}
else{
bars[i].smaFast=bars[i].close
}
if(i>=slowPeriod){
let sum=0;
for(let j=0;
j<slowPeriod;
j++)sum+=bars[i-j].close;
bars[i].smaSlow=Number((sum/slowPeriod).toFixed(4))
}
else{
bars[i].smaSlow=bars[i].close
}
if(i>=rsiPeriod){
let gains=0;
let losses=0;
for(let j=0;
j<rsiPeriod;
j++){
const diff=bars[i-j].close-bars[i-j-1].close;
if(diff>0)gains+=diff;
else losses-=diff
}
const val=gains+losses===0?50:100*(gains/(gains+losses));
bars[i].rsi=Number(val.toFixed(1))
}
else{
bars[i].rsi=50+bars[i].close%15-7.5
}
let currentEma5=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma5=bars[i].close*k5+lastEma5*(1-k5)
}
bars[i].ema5=Number(currentEma5.toFixed(4));
lastEma5=currentEma5;
let currentEma40=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma40=bars[i].close*k40+lastEma40*(1-k40)
}
lastEma40=currentEma40;
let currentEma200=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma200=bars[i].close*k200+lastEma200*(1-k200)
}
bars[i].ema200=Number(currentEma200.toFixed(4));
lastEma200=currentEma200;
let currentEma20=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma20=bars[i].close*k20+lastEma20*(1-k20)
}
bars[i].ema20=Number(currentEma20.toFixed(4));
lastEma20=currentEma20;
let currentEma50=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma50=bars[i].close*k50+lastEma50*(1-k50)
}
bars[i].ema50=Number(currentEma50.toFixed(4));
lastEma50=currentEma50;
let currentEma100=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma100=bars[i].close*k100+lastEma100*(1-k100)
}
bars[i].ema100=Number(currentEma100.toFixed(4));
lastEma100=currentEma100;
let currentEma9=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma9=bars[i].close*k9+lastEma9*(1-k9)
}
bars[i].ema9=Number(currentEma9.toFixed(4));
lastEma9=currentEma9;
let currentEma15=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma15=bars[i].close*k15+lastEma15*(1-k15)
}
bars[i].ema15=Number(currentEma15.toFixed(4));
lastEma15=currentEma15;
let currentWma40=bars[i].close;
if(i>=39){
let sum=0;
let weightSum=0;
for(let j=0;
j<40;
j++){
const weight=40-j;
sum+=bars[i-j].close*weight;
weightSum+=weight
}
currentWma40=sum/weightSum
}
const hybridRaw=.7*currentEma40+.3*currentWma40;
hybridRaws.push(hybridRaw);
let currentEma12=bars[i].close;
let currentEma26=bars[i].close;
if(i>0){
currentEma12=bars[i].close*k12+lastEma12*(1-k12);
currentEma26=bars[i].close*k26+lastEma26*(1-k26)
}
lastEma12=currentEma12;
lastEma26=currentEma26;
const macdLine=currentEma12-currentEma26;
let macdSig=macdLine;
if(i>0){
macdSig=macdLine*kSignal+lastEmaSignal*(1-kSignal)
}
lastEmaSignal=macdSig;
bars[i].macd=Number(macdLine.toFixed(5));
bars[i].macdSignal=Number(macdSig.toFixed(5));
bars[i].macdHist=Number((macdLine-macdSig).toFixed(5));
let tr=bars[i].high-bars[i].low;
if(i>0){
const prevClose=bars[i-1].close;
const tr1=bars[i].high-bars[i].low;
const tr2=Math.abs(bars[i].high-prevClose);
const tr3=Math.abs(bars[i].low-prevClose);
tr=Math.max(tr1,tr2,tr3)
}
if(i>=atrPeriod){
let trSum=0;
for(let j=0;
j<atrPeriod;
j++){
const idx=i-j;
let localTr=bars[idx].high-bars[idx].low;
if(idx>0){
const pc=bars[idx-1].close;
localTr=Math.max(bars[idx].high-bars[idx].low,Math.abs(bars[idx].high-pc),Math.abs(bars[idx].low-pc))
}
trSum+=localTr
}
bars[i].atr=Number((trSum/atrPeriod).toFixed(5))
}
else{
bars[i].atr=Number((bars[i].high-bars[i].low
  || bars[i].close*.0015).toFixed(5))
}
if(i>=bbPeriod){
let closeSum=0;
for(let j=0;
j<bbPeriod;
j++){
closeSum+=bars[i-j].close
}
const mean=closeSum/bbPeriod;
let varianceSum=0;
for(let j=0;
j<bbPeriod;
j++){
varianceSum+=Math.pow(bars[i-j].close-mean,2)
}
const variance=varianceSum/bbPeriod;
const stdDev=Math.sqrt(variance);
bars[i].bbMiddle=Number(mean.toFixed(4));
bars[i].bbUpper=Number((mean+2*stdDev).toFixed(4));
bars[i].bbLower=Number((mean-2*stdDev).toFixed(4))
}
else{
bars[i].bbMiddle=bars[i].close;
bars[i].bbUpper=Number((bars[i].close*1.01).toFixed(4));
bars[i].bbLower=Number((bars[i].close*.99).toFixed(4))
}

}
let lastActiveEma=hybridRaws[0];
for(let i=0;
i<bars.length;
i++){
let currentActiveEma=hybridRaws[i];
if(i>0){
currentActiveEma=hybridRaws[i]*k3+lastActiveEma*(1-k3)
}
bars[i].activeLine=Number(currentActiveEma.toFixed(4));
lastActiveEma=currentActiveEma
}
const medianPrices=bars.map(b=>(b.high+b.low)/2);
const smma8=[];
const smma5=[];
const smma3=[];
let sum8=0;
for(let i=0;
i<bars.length;
i++){
if(i<8){
sum8+=medianPrices[i];
if(i===7){
smma8.push(sum8/8)
}
else{
smma8.push(medianPrices[i])
}

}
else{
const prev=smma8[i-1];
smma8.push((prev*7+medianPrices[i])/8)
}

}
let sum5=0;
for(let i=0;
i<bars.length;
i++){
if(i<5){
sum5+=medianPrices[i];
if(i===4){
smma5.push(sum5/5)
}
else{
smma5.push(medianPrices[i])
}

}
else{
const prev=smma5[i-1];
smma5.push((prev*4+medianPrices[i])/5)
}

}
let sum3=0;
for(let i=0;
i<bars.length;
i++){
if(i<3){
sum3+=medianPrices[i];
if(i===2){
smma3.push(sum3/3)
}
else{
smma3.push(medianPrices[i])
}

}
else{
const prev=smma3[i-1];
smma3.push((prev*2+medianPrices[i])/3)
}

}
for(let i=0;
i<bars.length;
i++){
const jawIdx=i-5;
bars[i].alligatorJaw=jawIdx>=0?Number(smma8[jawIdx].toFixed(5)):Number(medianPrices[i].toFixed(5));
const teethIdx=i-3;
bars[i].alligatorTeeth=teethIdx>=0?Number(smma5[teethIdx].toFixed(5)):Number(medianPrices[i].toFixed(5));
const lipsIdx=i-2;
bars[i].alligatorLips=lipsIdx>=0?Number(smma3[lipsIdx].toFixed(5)):Number(medianPrices[i].toFixed(5))
}

}
__name(computeIndicators,"computeIndicators");

function generateStrongPasscode(){
const uppercase="ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ";
const lowercase="abcdefghijkmnopqrstuvwxyz";
const numbers="23456789";
const symbols="!@#%^*";
const all=uppercase+lowercase+numbers+symbols;
let passcode="QP-";
passcode+=uppercase[Math.floor(Math.random()*uppercase.length)];
passcode+=lowercase[Math.floor(Math.random()*lowercase.length)];
passcode+=numbers[Math.floor(Math.random()*numbers.length)];
passcode+=symbols[Math.floor(Math.random()*symbols.length)];
for(let i=0;
i<4;
i++){
passcode+=all[Math.floor(Math.random()*all.length)]
}
const chars=passcode.slice(3).split("");
for(let i=chars.length-1;
i>0;
i--){
const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));
[chars[i],chars[j]]=[chars[j],chars[i]]
}
return"QP-"+chars.join("")
}

function sanitizeFirestore(obj){
if(obj===void 0){
return null
}
if(obj===null){
return null
}
if(obj instanceof Date){
return obj.toISOString()
}
if(typeof obj==="function"){
return null
}
if(Array.isArray(obj)){
return obj.map(sanitizeFirestore)
}
if(typeof obj==="object"){
const res={

}
;
for(const key of Object.keys(obj)){
const val=obj[key];
if(val!==void 0
  && typeof val!=="function"){
res[key]=sanitizeFirestore(val)
}
else{
res[key]=null
}

}
return res
}
return obj
}
__name(sanitizeFirestore,"sanitizeFirestore");
function withTimeout(promise,timeoutMs=15e3){
let timer;
const timeoutPromise=new Promise((_,reject)=>{
timer=setTimeout(()=>{
reject(new Error(`Firebase operation timed out (${
timeoutMs
}
ms limit) due to connection throttling or quota exhaustion.`))
}
,timeoutMs)
}
);
return Promise.race([promise,timeoutPromise]).finally(()=>clearTimeout(timer))
}
__name(withTimeout,"withTimeout");

async function handleFirebaseError(err,context){
if(!firebaseEnabled)return;
const errorStr=String(err?.message
  || err?.code
  || err
  || "");
const isHardQuotaError=errorStr.includes("RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED")
  || errorStr.includes("quota")
  || errorStr.includes("Quota")
  || errorStr.includes("resource-exhausted")
  || errorStr.includes("limit exceeded")
  || errorStr.includes("exhausted")
  || errorStr.includes("timed out")
  || errorStr.includes("timeout")
  || errorStr.includes("Timeout")
  || err?.code==="resource-exhausted"
  || errorStr.includes("PERMISSION_DENIED")
  || errorStr.includes("permission-denied");
if(isHardQuotaError){
console.warn(`[Firebase Server] Expected quota limit / timeout / permissions issue during ${
context
}
: ${
errorStr
}
. Gracefully disabling Firestore and switching to local disk persistence.`);
await disableFirebaseGracefully();
return
}
firebaseConsecutiveErrors++;
console.warn(`[Firebase Server] Non-fatal Firebase warning #${
firebaseConsecutiveErrors
}
 during ${
context
}
: ${
errorStr
}
.`);
if(firebaseConsecutiveErrors>=10){
console.warn(`[Firebase Server] Too many consecutive transient connection timeouts/errors (${
firebaseConsecutiveErrors
}
). Gracefully disabling Firestore.`);
await disableFirebaseGracefully()
}

}
__name(handleFirebaseError,"handleFirebaseError");
async function disableFirebaseGracefully(){
if(firebaseEnabled){
console.warn("[Firebase Server] Firestore resource exhausted or connection issue. Gracefully falling back to high-performance disk-based persistence storage.");
firebaseEnabled=false;
const oldDb=db;
db=null;
if(oldDb){
try{
await terminate(oldDb);
console.log("[Firebase Server] Firestore connection terminated successfully to release active stream channels.")
}
catch(err){
console.warn("[Firebase Server] Non-blocking: Error while terminating Firestore client:",err)
}

}
if(isPrimary){
broadcastToWorkers()
}
else{
broadcastState()
}

}

}
__name(disableFirebaseGracefully,"disableFirebaseGracefully");
function getCollectionName(baseName){
return isProduction()?baseName:`${
baseName
}
_dev`
}
__name(getCollectionName,"getCollectionName");
async function saveStateToFirestore(onlyTenantId,instant){
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db)return;
if(!isFirestoreInitialLoadComplete){
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Bypassing saveStateToFirestore. Initial load from Firestore is still in progress...`);
return
}
const targetId=onlyTenantId
  || activeTenantId;
if(targetId){
tenantLastLocalWrite[targetId]=Date.now();
tenantStateIsDirty[targetId]=true
}
else{
Object.keys(tenants).forEach(tId=>{
tenantLastLocalWrite[tId]=Date.now();
tenantStateIsDirty[tId]=true
}
)
}
if(instant){
if(!targetId
  || !tenants[targetId])return;
try{
const t=tenants[targetId];
const rawTrades=t.state.trades
  || [];
const openTrades=rawTrades.filter(x=>x.status==="OPEN");
const closedTrades=rawTrades.filter(x=>x.status!=="OPEN");
const trimmedTrades=[...openTrades,...closedTrades.slice(0,10)];
const trimmedSignals=(t.state.signals
  || []).slice(-10);
const trimmedAlerts=(t.state.telegramAlerts
  || []).slice(-5);
const trimmedRetests=(t.state.retestCandidates
  || []).slice(-5);
const tenantPayload={
name:t.name,email:t.email,username:t.username,password:t.password,passcode:t.passcode
  || t.password,tier:t.tier,status:t.status,price:t.price,nextBillingDate:t.nextBillingDate,limits:t.limits,disabledMenus:t.disabledMenus
  || [],stripeCustomerId:t.stripeCustomerId
  || "",stripeSubscriptionId:t.stripeSubscriptionId
  || "",stripeCurrentPeriodEnd:t.stripeCurrentPeriodEnd
  || 0,state:{
config:t.state.config,strategies:t.state.strategies,trades:trimmedTrades,signals:trimmedSignals,telegramAlerts:trimmedAlerts,news:t.state.news
  || [],mt5Config:t.state.mt5Config,aiNewsInsight:t.state.aiNewsInsight
  || "",retestCandidates:trimmedRetests
}
,id:targetId,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()
}
;
const tenantDocRef=doc(db,getCollectionName("tenants"),targetId);
await withTimeout(setDoc(tenantDocRef,sanitizeFirestore(tenantPayload)),30e3);
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Instant write successful for tenant: ${
targetId
}
`);
tenantStateIsDirty[targetId]=false;
tenantLastLocalWrite[targetId]=Date.parse(tenantPayload.updatedAt)
}
catch(err){
console.error(`[Firebase Server] Instant write failed for tenant: ${
targetId
}
:`,err)
}
return
}
if(targetId){
pendingTenantsToSave.add(targetId)
}
else{
Object.keys(tenants).forEach(tId=>pendingTenantsToSave.add(tId))
}
if(isSavingToFirestore){
return
}
if(firestoreWriteTimeoutId){
clearTimeout(firestoreWriteTimeoutId)
}
firestoreWriteTimeoutId=setTimeout(async()=>{
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db
  || isSavingToFirestore)return;
isSavingToFirestore=true;
const tenantsToSave=Array.from(pendingTenantsToSave);
pendingTenantsToSave.clear();
const filteredTenantsToSave=tenantsToSave.filter(tId=>{
if(!tenantStateIsDirty[tId]){
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Skipping debounced write for clean, idle tenant: "${
tId
}
"`);
return false
}
return true
}
);
if(filteredTenantsToSave.length===0){
console.log(`[Firebase Server] State sync skipped in background timeout: No tenants are dirty since the last sync.`);
isSavingToFirestore=false;
return
}
compactAndCleanState();
purgeExpiredFirestoreLogs().catch(e=>{
handleFirebaseError(e,"database sweep during logging").catch(()=>{

}
)
}
);
try{
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db)return;
const metadataDocRef=doc(db,getCollectionName("global_state"),"metadata");
await withTimeout(setDoc(metadataDocRef,sanitizeFirestore({
activeTenantId,pricingConfig,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()
}
)),30e3);
const promises=filteredTenantsToSave.map(async tId=>{
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db)return;
const t=tenants[tId];
if(!t)return;
try{
const tenantDocRef2=doc(db,getCollectionName("tenants"),tId);
const cloudSnap=await withTimeout(getDoc(tenantDocRef2),30e3);
if(cloudSnap.exists()){
const cloudData=cloudSnap.data();
const cloudUpdatedAt=cloudData.updatedAt?new Date(cloudData.updatedAt).getTime():0;
const localLastWrite=tenantLastLocalWrite[tId]
  || 0;
if(cloudData.state
  && cloudUpdatedAt>localLastWrite){
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Debounced save merged newer cloud config/strategies for tenant: ${
tId
}
`);
if(cloudData.state.config){
t.state.config={
...t.state.config,...cloudData.state.config
}

}
if(cloudData.state.strategies){
t.state.strategies=cloudData.state.strategies
}
tenantLastLocalWrite[tId]=cloudUpdatedAt
}

}

}
catch(err){
console.warn(`[Firebase Server] Non-blocking: Pre-save cloud merge failed for tenant: ${
tId
}
:`,err)
}
const rawTrades=t.state.trades
  || [];
const openTrades=rawTrades.filter(x=>x.status==="OPEN");
const closedTrades=rawTrades.filter(x=>x.status!=="OPEN");
const trimmedTrades=[...openTrades,...closedTrades.slice(0,10)];
const trimmedSignals=(t.state.signals
  || []).slice(-10);
const trimmedAlerts=(t.state.telegramAlerts
  || []).slice(-5);
const trimmedRetests=(t.state.retestCandidates
  || []).slice(-5);
const tenantPayload={
name:t.name,email:t.email,username:t.username,password:t.password,passcode:t.passcode
  || t.password,tier:t.tier,status:t.status,price:t.price,nextBillingDate:t.nextBillingDate,limits:t.limits,stripeCustomerId:t.stripeCustomerId
  || "",stripeSubscriptionId:t.stripeSubscriptionId
  || "",stripeCurrentPeriodEnd:t.stripeCurrentPeriodEnd
  || 0,state:{
config:t.state.config,strategies:t.state.strategies,trades:trimmedTrades,signals:trimmedSignals,telegramAlerts:trimmedAlerts,news:t.state.news
  || [],mt5Config:t.state.mt5Config,aiNewsInsight:t.state.aiNewsInsight
  || "",retestCandidates:trimmedRetests
}
,id:tId,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()
}
;
const tenantDocRef=doc(db,getCollectionName("tenants"),tId);
await withTimeout(setDoc(tenantDocRef,sanitizeFirestore(tenantPayload)),30e3);
tenantStateIsDirty[tId]=false;
tenantLastLocalWrite[tId]=Date.parse(tenantPayload.updatedAt)
}
);
await Promise.all(promises);
firebaseConsecutiveErrors=0;
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Successfully synced/debounced multi-tenant states to Firestore for tenants: ${
tenantsToSave.join(", ")
}
`)
}
catch(err){
tenantsToSave.forEach(tId=>pendingTenantsToSave.add(tId));
await handleFirebaseError(err,"saving state to Firestore")
}
finally{
isSavingToFirestore=false;
if(pendingTenantsToSave.size>0){
saveStateToFirestore()
}

}

}
,2500)
}
__name(saveStateToFirestore,"saveStateToFirestore");
async function saveDemoLogToFirestore(logData){
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db)return;
pendingDemoLogsToSave.set(logData.id,logData);
if(isSavingDemoLogs){
return
}
if(demoLogWriteTimeoutId){
clearTimeout(demoLogWriteTimeoutId)
}
demoLogWriteTimeoutId=setTimeout(async()=>{
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db
  || isSavingDemoLogs)return;
isSavingDemoLogs=true;
const logsToSave=Array.from(pendingDemoLogsToSave.values());
pendingDemoLogsToSave.clear();
try{
const promises=logsToSave.map(async log=>{
const logDocRef=doc(db,getCollectionName("demo_access_logs"),log.id);
await withTimeout(setDoc(logDocRef,sanitizeFirestore(log)),30e3)
}
);
await Promise.all(promises);
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Successfully synchronized ${
logsToSave.length
}
 throttled/debounced demo access logs directly to Firestore.`)
}
catch(err){
logsToSave.forEach(log=>pendingDemoLogsToSave.set(log.id,log));
await handleFirebaseError(err,"saving throttled demo access logs to Firestore")
}
finally{
isSavingDemoLogs=false
}

}
,3e4)
}
__name(saveDemoLogToFirestore,"saveDemoLogToFirestore");
function compactAndCleanState(){
console.log("[Compaction] Purging and compacting old, unused, temporary state histories in memory...");
try{
const today=new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
demoAccessLogs=(demoAccessLogs
  || []).filter(log=>log
  && (log.date===today
  || log.createdAt
  && log.createdAt.startsWith(today))).slice(-50);
Object.keys(tenants).forEach(tId=>{
const t=tenants[tId];
if(t
  && t.state){
if(t.state.config){
let balVal=Number(t.state.config.balance
  || 0);
if(balVal<1e3
  && balVal<999999999999){
const initialBalStr=balVal.toLocaleString(void 0,{
maximumFractionDigits:2
}
);
while(balVal<1e3){
balVal+=1e4
}
t.state.config.balance=balVal;
const openPnL=(t.state.trades
  || []).filter(tr=>tr
  && tr.status==="OPEN").reduce((sum,tr)=>sum+(tr.pnl
  || 0),0);
t.state.config.equity=Number((t.state.config.balance+openPnL).toFixed(2));
if(t.state.mt5Config){
if(!t.state.mt5Config.logs){
t.state.mt5Config.logs=[]
}
t.state.mt5Config.logs.unshift(`[Capital Assurance] Equity & Sizing safety guard triggered! Capital was $${
initialBalStr
}
, automatically added $10,000 top-ups. New Trading Capital: $${
balVal.toLocaleString(void 0,{
maximumFractionDigits:2
}
)
}
 USD.`)
}

}

}
const allTrades=t.state.trades
  || [];
const open=allTrades.filter(tr=>tr
  && tr.status==="OPEN");
const closed=allTrades.filter(tr=>tr
  && tr.status!=="OPEN");
closed.sort((a,b)=>{
const d1=new Date(b.closedAt
  || b.createdAt
  || 0).getTime();
const d2=new Date(a.closedAt
  || a.createdAt
  || 0).getTime();
return d1-d2
}
);
t.state.trades=[...open,...closed.slice(0,10)];
const signals=t.state.signals
  || [];
t.state.signals=signals.slice(-10);
const alerts=t.state.telegramAlerts
  || [];
t.state.telegramAlerts=alerts.slice(-5);
if(t.state.news){
t.state.news=ensureRollingNews(t.state.news);
t.state.news=t.state.news.slice(-20)
}
if(t.state.retestCandidates){
t.state.retestCandidates=t.state.retestCandidates.slice(-5)
}
if(t.state.mt5Config){
if(t.state.mt5Config.logs
  && Array.isArray(t.state.mt5Config.logs)){
t.state.mt5Config.logs=t.state.mt5Config.logs.slice(0,10)
}
if(t.state.mt5Config.mirrorActivity
  && Array.isArray(t.state.mt5Config.mirrorActivity)){
t.state.mt5Config.mirrorActivity=t.state.mt5Config.mirrorActivity.slice(0,10)
}

}
if(t.state.mlHistory
  && Array.isArray(t.state.mlHistory)){
t.state.mlHistory=t.state.mlHistory.slice(0,3)
}
if(t.state.mlRollbackPoints
  && Array.isArray(t.state.mlRollbackPoints)){
t.state.mlRollbackPoints=t.state.mlRollbackPoints.slice(0,3)
}
if(t.state.mlRecommendations
  && Array.isArray(t.state.mlRecommendations)){
t.state.mlRecommendations=t.state.mlRecommendations.slice(0,5)
}

}

}
);
console.log("[Compaction] In-memory multi-tenant state compacted successfully.")
}
catch(err){
console.error("[Compaction] Error during state compaction:",err)
}

}
__name(compactAndCleanState,"compactAndCleanState");
async function purgeExpiredFirestoreLogs(){
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db)return;
const now=Date.now();
if(now-lastLogSweepTime<6*60*60*1e3){
return
}
lastLogSweepTime=now;
try{
console.log("[Firebase Server] Initiating background Firestore log database sweep...");
const todayStr=new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
const logsCollectionRef=collection(db,getCollectionName("demo_access_logs"));
const snap=await withTimeout(getDocs(logsCollectionRef),30e3);
let deletedCount=0;
const deletePromises=[];
snap.forEach(docSnap=>{
const data=docSnap.data();
if(data
  && data.date!==todayStr){
const docRef=docSnap.ref;
deletePromises.push(deleteDoc(docRef).then(()=>{
deletedCount++
}
).catch(()=>{

}
))
}

}
);
if(deletePromises.length>0){
await Promise.all(deletePromises);
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Database Sweep Complete: Purged ${
deletedCount
}
 unused historical demo access log documents.`)
}
else{
console.log("[Firebase Server] Database Sweep: No expired audit logs found in Firestore.")
}

}
catch(err){
console.warn("[Firebase Server] Non-blocking database sweep encountered an exception:",err?.message
  || err);
await handleFirebaseError(err,"database sweep")
}

}
__name(purgeExpiredFirestoreLogs,"purgeExpiredFirestoreLogs");
async function loadDemoLogsFromFirestore(){
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db)return;
try{
const todayStr=new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
const logsCollectionRef=collection(db,getCollectionName("demo_access_logs"));
const logsQuery=query(logsCollectionRef,where("date","==",todayStr));
const snap=await withTimeout(getDocs(logsQuery),30e3);
const loaded=[];
snap.forEach(docSnap=>{
const data=docSnap.data();
if(data){
loaded.push(data)
}

}
);
demoAccessLogs=loaded;
firebaseConsecutiveErrors=0;
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Loaded ${
demoAccessLogs.length
}
 active demo access logs for today (${
todayStr
}
) from Firestore.`)
}
catch(err){
await handleFirebaseError(err,"fetching demo access logs")
}

}
__name(loadDemoLogsFromFirestore,"loadDemoLogsFromFirestore");
async function loadStateFromFirestore(onlyTenantId){
if(!firebaseEnabled
  || !db)return"disabled";
try{
if(!onlyTenantId){
const metadataDocRef=doc(db,getCollectionName("global_state"),"metadata");
const metaSnap=await withTimeout(getDoc(metadataDocRef),30e3);
if(metaSnap.exists()){
const metaData=metaSnap.data();
if(metaData.activeTenantId){
activeTenantId=metaData.activeTenantId
}
if(metaData.pricingConfig){
pricingConfig=metaData.pricingConfig
}

}

}
const processSavedTenant=__name((tId,savedT)=>{
if(!savedT)return;
const dbUpdatedAt=savedT.updatedAt?new Date(savedT.updatedAt).getTime():0;
const localLastModified=tenantLastLocalWrite[tId]
  || 0;
if(localLastModified>0
  && dbUpdatedAt<=localLastModified){
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Bypassing cloud state overwrite for tenant "${
tId
}
". Local memory state is equal to or newer than cloud data (local=${
new Date(localLastModified).toISOString()
}
 >= db=${
savedT.updatedAt
  || "none"
}
).`);
return
}
if(!tenants[tId]){
tenants[tId]={
id:tId,name:savedT.name
  || "Custom Operator",email:savedT.email
  || "operator@customdomain.com",username:savedT.username
  || tId.replace("tenant-",""),password:savedT.passcode
  || savedT.password
  || "@Hariom12",passcode:savedT.passcode
  || savedT.password
  || "@Hariom12",tier:savedT.tier
  || "Starter",status:savedT.status
  || "Active",price:savedT.price
  || 99,nextBillingDate:savedT.nextBillingDate
  || new Date(Date.now()+30*24*3600*1e3).toISOString().split("T")[0],limits:savedT.limits
  || {
maxStrategies:3,maxLotSize:.5,maxConcurrentTrades:3,aiSignalsAllowed:false
}
,stripeCustomerId:savedT.stripeCustomerId
  || "",stripeSubscriptionId:savedT.stripeSubscriptionId
  || "",stripeCurrentPeriodEnd:savedT.stripeCurrentPeriodEnd
  || 0,state:deepClone(state)
}

}
if(tenants[tId]){
tenants[tId].name=savedT.name
  || tenants[tId].name;
tenants[tId].email=savedT.email
  || tenants[tId].email;
tenants[tId].username=savedT.username
  || tenants[tId].username
  || tId.replace("tenant-","");
tenants[tId].password=savedT.passcode
  || savedT.password
  || tenants[tId].passcode
  || tenants[tId].password
  || "@Hariom12";
tenants[tId].passcode=tenants[tId].password;
tenants[tId].tier=savedT.tier
  || tenants[tId].tier;
tenants[tId].status=savedT.status
  || tenants[tId].status;
tenants[tId].price=savedT.price
  || tenants[tId].price;
tenants[tId].nextBillingDate=savedT.nextBillingDate
  || tenants[tId].nextBillingDate;
tenants[tId].limits=savedT.limits
  || tenants[tId].limits;
tenants[tId].disabledMenus=savedT.disabledMenus
  || [];
tenants[tId].stripeCustomerId=savedT.stripeCustomerId
  || "";
tenants[tId].stripeSubscriptionId=savedT.stripeSubscriptionId
  || "";
tenants[tId].stripeCurrentPeriodEnd=savedT.stripeCurrentPeriodEnd
  || 0;
if(savedT.state){
const tState=tenants[tId].state;
if(savedT.state.config)tState.config={
...tState.config,...savedT.state.config
}
;
if(savedT.state.strategies){
const incoming=savedT.state.strategies
  || [];
const merged=[...tState.strategies
  || []];
incoming.forEach(inS=>{
const idx=merged.findIndex(x=>x.id===inS.id);
if(idx!==-1){
merged[idx]={
...merged[idx],...inS
}

}
else{
merged.push(inS)
}

}
);
tState.strategies=merged
}
if(savedT.state.trades)tState.trades=savedT.state.trades;
if(savedT.state.signals)tState.signals=savedT.state.signals;
if(savedT.state.telegramAlerts)tState.telegramAlerts=savedT.state.telegramAlerts;
if(savedT.state.news)tState.news=savedT.state.news;
if(savedT.state.mt5Config)tState.mt5Config={
...tState.mt5Config,...savedT.state.mt5Config
}
;
if(savedT.state.aiNewsInsight)tState.aiNewsInsight=savedT.state.aiNewsInsight;
if(savedT.state.retestCandidates)tState.retestCandidates=savedT.state.retestCandidates;
migrateLoadedTenantState(tState)
}

}
tenantLastLocalWrite[tId]=dbUpdatedAt
}
,"processSavedTenant");
if(onlyTenantId){
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Direct load requested for selective occupant: "${
onlyTenantId
}
"`);
const tenantDocRef=doc(db,getCollectionName("tenants"),onlyTenantId);
const docSnap=await withTimeout(getDoc(tenantDocRef),30e3);
if(docSnap.exists()){
processSavedTenant(onlyTenantId,docSnap.data())
}

}
else{
const tenantsQuery=collection(db,getCollectionName("tenants"));
const snap=await withTimeout(getDocs(tenantsQuery),30e3);
if(snap.empty){
console.log("[Firebase Server] No tenants found in Firestore yet. Operating with local defaults.");
return"empty"
}
snap.forEach(docSnap=>{
processSavedTenant(docSnap.id,docSnap.data())
}
)
}
await loadDemoLogsFromFirestore();
firebaseConsecutiveErrors=0;
console.log(`[Firebase Server] Successfully restored state from Firestore (filter=${
onlyTenantId
  || "all"
}
).`);
return"success"
}
catch(err){
await handleFirebaseError(err,"loading state from Firestore");
return"error"
}

}
__name(loadStateFromFirestore,"loadStateFromFirestore");

function saveState(){
compactAndCleanState();
try{
const backup={
activeTenantId,pricingConfig,tenants:{

}

}
;
Object.keys(tenants).forEach(tId=>{
const t=tenants[tId];
backup.tenants[tId]={
name:t.name,email:t.email,username:t.username,password:t.password,passcode:t.passcode
  || t.password,tier:t.tier,status:t.status,price:t.price,nextBillingDate:t.nextBillingDate,limits:t.limits,disabledMenus:t.disabledMenus
  || [],stripeCustomerId:t.stripeCustomerId
  || "",stripeSubscriptionId:t.stripeSubscriptionId
  || "",stripeCurrentPeriodEnd:t.stripeCurrentPeriodEnd
  || 0,state:{
config:t.state.config,strategies:t.state.strategies,trades:t.state.trades,signals:t.state.signals,telegramAlerts:t.state.telegramAlerts,news:t.state.news,mt5Config:t.state.mt5Config,aiNewsInsight:t.state.aiNewsInsight,retestCandidates:t.state.retestCandidates
}

}

}
);
fs.writeFileSync(STATE_FILE,JSON.stringify(backup,null,2),"utf-8");
if(isPrimary){
broadcastToWorkers()
}
else{
broadcastState()
}

}
catch(err){
console.error("[State Persistence] Failed to save multi-tenant state locally:",err)
}

}
__name(saveState,"saveState");
function broadcastToWorkers(){
if(isPrimary
  && cluster.workers){
const payload={
tenants,activeTenantId,pricingConfig,demoAccessLogs,latestLivePrices,firebaseEnabled
}
;
for(const id in cluster.workers){
const worker=cluster.workers[id];
if(worker
  && worker.isConnected()){
worker.send({
type:"SYNC_STATE",payload
}
)
}

}

}

}

}

}
)
}

}

async function fetchBatchLivePrices(){
try{
const symbolMap={
"BTC-USD":"BTCUSD","ETH-USD":"ETHUSD","SOL-USD":"SOLUSD","BNB-USD":"BNBUSD","EURUSD=X":"EURUSD","GBPUSD=X":"GBPUSD","USDJPY=X":"USDJPY","AUDUSD=X":"AUDUSD","GC=F":"XAUUSD","CL=F":"USOIL","SI=F":"XAGUSD","NG=F":"NGAS","AAPL":"AAPL","TSLA":"TSLA","MSFT":"MSFT","NVDA":"NVDA","^GSPC":"SPX500","^NDX":"NDX100","^DJI":"DJI30","^GDAXI":"GER40"
}
;
const symbolsQuery=Object.keys(symbolMap).join(",");
const res=await fetchWithTimeout(`https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v7/finance/quote?symbols=${
symbolsQuery
}
`,{
headers:{
"User-Agent":"Mozilla/5.0"
}

}
);
if(!res.ok){
console.warn(`[Price Sync] Batch fetch response not OK: ${
res.status
}
`);
return
}
const data=await res.json();
const resultList=data?.quoteResponse?.result;
if(!Array.isArray(resultList))return;
resultList.forEach(item=>{
const liveSymbol=item.symbol;
const livePrice=item.regularMarketPrice;
const appSymbol=symbolMap[liveSymbol];
if(appSymbol
  && livePrice
  && !isNaN(livePrice)){
latestLivePrices[appSymbol]=livePrice;
BASE_PRICES[appSymbol]=livePrice
}

}
)
}
catch(err){
if(err
  && err.name==="AbortError"){
console.warn("[Price Sync] Batch live quote fetch timed out (Yahoo Finance unresponsive). Using simulated updates.")
}
else{
console.error("[Price Sync] Batch live quote fetch error:",err)
}

}

}

}

if(tickCount%100===0){

}
const shouldRollBar=tickCount%8===0;
SYMBOLS.forEach(sym=>{
if(!isPairEnabled(sym))return;
const data=state.marketData[sym];
const prevPrice=data.currentPrice;
let driftBias=0;
const hasPullbackOpt=state.config.activeOptimizations?.["ins-entry-pullback-depth"];
const hasAdaptiveOpt=state.config.activeOptimizations?.["ins-adaptive-sltp"];
if(hasPullbackOpt
  || hasAdaptiveOpt){
const openTrades=state.trades.filter(t2=>t2.symbol===sym
  && t2.status==="OPEN");
let buyCount=0;
let sellCount=0;
openTrades.forEach(t2=>{
if(t2.type==="BUY")buyCount++;
else if(t2.type==="SELL")sellCount++
}
);
if(buyCount>sellCount){
driftBias=.08
}
else if(sellCount>buyCount){
driftBias=-.08
}

}
let newPrice;
if(latestLivePrices[sym]){
const target=latestLivePrices[sym];
const jitter=(Math.random()-.5+driftBias)*(sym==="BTCUSD"?4:sym==="SPX500"?.25:sym==="XAUUSD"?.15:sym==="AAPL"?.03:15e-5);

  && !sym.startsWith("XAU")
  && !sym.startsWith("BTC")?4:2))

else{

newPrice=Number((prevPrice*(1+changePct)).toFixed(sym.includes("USD")
  && !sym.startsWith("XAU")
  && !sym.startsWith("BTC")?4:2))
}
data.currentPrice=newPrice;

  && history.length>0){

time:newTime,open:lastBar.close,high:lastBar.close,low:lastBar.close,close:lastBar.close,volume:0
}
;

history.shift()
}
