__name(checkNewsBlock,"checkNewsBlock");
function checkSessionBoundaryBlock(windowMinutes=30){
const now=new Date;
const currentMinutesSinceMidnight=now.getUTCHours()*60+now.getUTCMinutes();
const boundaries=[0,8,13,16,21];
for(const bHour of boundaries){
const bMinutes=bHour*60;
let diff=Math.abs(currentMinutesSinceMidnight-bMinutes);
if(diff>12*60){
diff=24*60-diff
}
if(diff<=windowMinutes){
return{
blocked:true,targetHour:bHour,timeDiffMinutes:diff
}

}

}
return{
blocked:false
}

}
__name(checkSessionBoundaryBlock,"checkSessionBoundaryBlock");
function evaluateUnifiedConfluence(symbol,direction,isExitEvaluation,trade){
const defaultRes={
score:0,breakdown:{
structure:0,priceAction:0,momentum:0,volume:0,volatility:0
}
,details:isExitEvaluation?"ITME analysis not active or insufficient history":"Market study initial scans pending"
}
;
if(!state
  || !state.marketData)return defaultRes;
const asset=state.marketData[symbol];
if(!asset
  || !asset.history
  || asset.history.length<15)return defaultRes;
const history=asset.history;
const currentPrice=asset.currentPrice;
const lastBar=history[history.length-1];
const prevBar=history[history.length-2];
const thirdBar=history[history.length-3];
const isBuy=direction==="BUY";
const wStructure=state.config.itmeWeightStructure!==void 0?state.config.itmeWeightStructure:20;
const wPriceAction=state.config.itmeWeightPriceAction!==void 0?state.config.itmeWeightPriceAction:20;
const wMomentum=state.config.itmeWeightMomentum!==void 0?state.config.itmeWeightMomentum:20;
const wVolume=state.config.itmeWeightVolume!==void 0?state.config.itmeWeightVolume:20;
const wVolatility=state.config.itmeWeightVolatility!==void 0?state.config.itmeWeightVolatility:20;
const totalWeight=wStructure+wPriceAction+wMomentum+wVolume+wVolatility
  || 100;
const last10Bars=history.slice(-10);
const activeLine=lastBar.activeLine
  || currentPrice;
const smaFast=lastBar.smaFast
  || currentPrice;
const smaSlow=lastBar.smaSlow
  || currentPrice;
const currentRsi=lastBar.rsi
  || 50;
const prevRsi=prevBar.rsi
  || 50;
const macdHist=lastBar.macdHist
  || 0;
const prevMacdHist=prevBar.macdHist
  || 0;
const avgVol=last10Bars.reduce((sum,b)=>sum+(b.volume
  || 100),0)/10;
const lastVol=lastBar.volume
  || 100;
if(isExitEvaluation
  && trade){
const lookbackBars=history.filter(b=>new Date(b.time).getTime()>=new Date(trade.openTime).getTime());
const entryPrice=trade.entryPrice;
let peakPrice=entryPrice;
if(isBuy){
const historicalHighs=lookbackBars.map(b=>b.high);
peakPrice=Math.max(entryPrice,currentPrice,...historicalHighs)
}
else{
const historicalLows=lookbackBars.map(b=>b.low);
peakPrice=Math.min(entryPrice,currentPrice,...historicalLows)
}
const swingSize=isBuy?peakPrice-entryPrice:entryPrice-peakPrice;
const adverseMovement=isBuy?peakPrice-currentPrice:currentPrice-peakPrice;
const fibRetracement=swingSize>0?adverseMovement/swingSize:0;
let structureScore=0;
if(isBuy){
if(smaFast<smaSlow)structureScore+=30;
if(currentPrice<activeLine)structureScore+=20;
const lowestLowLast10=Math.min(...last10Bars.map(b=>b.low));
if(currentPrice<lowestLowLast10){
structureScore+=50
}

}
else{
if(smaFast>smaSlow)structureScore+=30;
if(currentPrice>activeLine)structureScore+=20;
const highestHighLast10=Math.max(...last10Bars.map(b=>b.high));
if(currentPrice>highestHighLast10){
structureScore+=50
}

}
let priceActionScore=0;
if(isBuy){
const isBearishEngulfing=prevBar.close>prevBar.open
  && lastBar.close<lastBar.open
  && lastBar.close<=prevBar.open
  && lastBar.open>=prevBar.close;
if(isBearishEngulfing)priceActionScore+=40;
const last3Bearish=lastBar.close<lastBar.open
  && prevBar.close<prevBar.open
  && thirdBar.close<thirdBar.open;
const last2Bearish=lastBar.close<lastBar.open
  && prevBar.close<prevBar.open;
if(last3Bearish)priceActionScore+=40;
else if(last2Bearish)priceActionScore+=20;
const lastBodySize=Math.abs(lastBar.close-lastBar.open);
const topWick=lastBar.high-Math.max(lastBar.open,lastBar.close);
if(topWick>lastBodySize*2
  && topWick>0){
priceActionScore+=20
}

}
else{
const isBullishEngulfing=prevBar.close<prevBar.open
  && lastBar.close>lastBar.open
  && lastBar.close>=prevBar.open
  && lastBar.open<=prevBar.close;
if(isBullishEngulfing)priceActionScore+=40;
const last3Bullish=lastBar.close>lastBar.open
  && prevBar.close>prevBar.open
  && thirdBar.close>thirdBar.open;
const last2Bullish=lastBar.close>lastBar.open
  && prevBar.close>prevBar.open;
if(last3Bullish)priceActionScore+=40;
else if(last2Bullish)priceActionScore+=20;
const lastBodySize=Math.abs(lastBar.close-lastBar.open);
const bottomWick=Math.min(lastBar.open,lastBar.close)-lastBar.low;
if(bottomWick>lastBodySize*2
  && bottomWick>0){
priceActionScore+=20
}

}
let momentumScore=0;
if(isBuy){
if(prevRsi>60
  && currentRsi<50)momentumScore+=40;
else if(currentRsi<45)momentumScore+=20;
if(macdHist<0
  && prevMacdHist>=0){
momentumScore+=50
}
else if(macdHist<prevMacdHist){
momentumScore+=20
}

}
else{
if(prevRsi<40
  && currentRsi>50)momentumScore+=40;
else if(currentRsi>55)momentumScore+=20;
if(macdHist>0
  && prevMacdHist<=0){
momentumScore+=50
}
else if(macdHist>prevMacdHist){
momentumScore+=20
}

}
let volumeScore=0;
const isOpposingMove=isBuy?lastBar.close<lastBar.open:lastBar.close>lastBar.open;
if(isOpposingMove){
if(lastVol>avgVol*1.8){
volumeScore+=100
}
else if(lastVol>avgVol*1.3){
volumeScore+=60
}
else{
volumeScore+=30
}

}
let volatilityScore=0;
const sampleAtr=lastBar.atr
  || currentPrice*.002;
const adversePriceDiff=isBuy?trade.entryPrice-currentPrice:currentPrice-trade.entryPrice;
const adverseAtrRatio=adversePriceDiff/sampleAtr;
if(isBuy){
if(currentPrice<(lastBar.bbLower
  || currentPrice)){
volatilityScore+=50
}

}
else{
if(currentPrice>(lastBar.bbUpper
  || currentPrice)){
volatilityScore+=50
}

}
if(adverseAtrRatio>1.5){
volatilityScore+=50
}
else if(adverseAtrRatio>.8){
volatilityScore+=25
}
let isNormalPullback=false;
let pullbackDampeningFactor=1;
let classifiedReason="";
if(state.config.itmeFilterPullbacks){
const opposingVolumeSpike=isOpposingMove
  && lastVol>avgVol*1.25;
const hasBrokenSupport=isBuy?currentPrice<lastBar.bbMiddle:currentPrice>lastBar.bbMiddle;
if(adverseAtrRatio<.95
  && fibRetracement<.5
  && !opposingVolumeSpike
  && !hasBrokenSupport){
isNormalPullback=true;
pullbackDampeningFactor=.25;
classifiedReason="\u{
1F6E1
}
\uFE0F PULLBACK GUARD: Non-threatening retracement (Fib: "+(fibRetracement*100).toFixed(0)+"%, Vol: "+(lastVol/avgVol).toFixed(1)+"x). Hold thesis is active to avoid premature flip."
}
else if(fibRetracement>.618
  && (opposingVolumeSpike
  || adverseAtrRatio>1.2)){
classifiedReason="\u{
1F6A8
}
 TREND REVERSAL: Confirmed trend breakdown. Deep swing breakout (Fib: "+(fibRetracement*100).toFixed(0)+"%, Vol: "+(lastVol/avgVol).toFixed(1)+"x). Proactive risk intervention recommended."
}
else{
classifiedReason="\u26A0\uFE0F INTERMEDIATE CONFLICT: Modest counter-forces observed. Vol: "+(lastVol/avgVol).toFixed(1)+"x. Scanning momentum confluences."
}

}
else{
classifiedReason="ITME monitoring active. Adaptive filters bypassed by system operator."
}
let weightedScoreRaw=(structureScore*wStructure+priceActionScore*wPriceAction+momentumScore*wMomentum+volumeScore*wVolume+volatilityScore*wVolatility)/totalWeight;
if(isNormalPullback){
weightedScoreRaw=weightedScoreRaw*pullbackDampeningFactor;
structureScore*=.3;
priceActionScore*=.3;
momentumScore*=.2;
volumeScore*=.2;
volatilityScore=0
}
const finalScore=Math.min(100,Math.max(0,Math.round(weightedScoreRaw)));
let detailsText="";
if(isNormalPullback){
detailsText=`${
classifiedReason
}
 Low threat anti-thesis score damped to ${
finalScore
}
%. Position is protected from premature exits.`
}
else if(finalScore>=(state.config.itmeThreshold
  || 75)){
detailsText=`${
classifiedReason
}
 ITME anti-thesis score is critical at ${
finalScore
}
%. Signals verified: Structure mismatch (${
Math.round(structureScore)
}
%), Price degradation (${
Math.round(priceActionScore)
}
%), Opposing Volume (${
Math.round(volumeScore)
}
%), Volatility break (${
Math.round(volatilityScore)
}
%). Proactive liquidation triggered @ $${
currentPrice.toFixed(2)
}
.`
}
else if(finalScore>40){
detailsText=`${
classifiedReason
}
 Moderate counter-pressures at ${
finalScore
}
%. Indicator confluences aren't critical. Holding position.`
}
else{
detailsText=`\u2728 THESIS INTACT: Anti-thesis score is negligible at ${
finalScore
}
%. Trend direction remains favorable. Bull/Bear momentum aligned.`
}
return{
score:finalScore,breakdown:{
structure:Math.round(structureScore),priceAction:Math.round(priceActionScore),momentum:Math.round(momentumScore),volume:Math.round(volumeScore),volatility:Math.round(volatilityScore)
}
,details:detailsText
}

}
else{
const currentRegime=asset.regime
  || "RANGING_CHOP";
const ema200Val=lastBar.ema200
  || currentPrice;
const dailyChg=asset.dailyChange!==void 0?asset.dailyChange:.005;
const htf1HAlign=isBuy?smaFast>smaSlow?9:2:smaFast<smaSlow?9:2;
const htfDailyBias=isBuy?dailyChg>0?9:2:dailyChg<0?9:2;
const htfSRClearance=isBuy?currentPrice<lastBar.bbUpper*.998?7:1.5:currentPrice>lastBar.bbLower*1.002?7:1.5;
const htfAlignmentScore=Number((htf1HAlign+htfDailyBias+htfSRClearance).toFixed(2));
const highestHighLast10=Math.max(...last10Bars.map(b=>b.high));
const lowestLowLast10=Math.min(...last10Bars.map(b=>b.low));
let structureBOS=2;
if(isBuy){
if(currentPrice>=highestHighLast10*.998)structureBOS=9
}
else{
if(currentPrice<=lowestLowLast10*1.002)structureBOS=9
}
const structureCHOCH=isBuy?smaFast>smaSlow?8:2:smaFast<smaSlow?8:2;
const structureSR=isBuy?currentPrice<lastBar.bbMiddle?8:2.5:currentPrice>lastBar.bbMiddle?8:2.5;
const marketStructureScore=Number((structureBOS+structureCHOCH+structureSR).toFixed(2));
let marketRegimeScore=5;
if(isBuy){
if(currentRegime==="TRENDING_BULL"
  || currentRegime==="ACCUMULATION")marketRegimeScore=20;
else if(currentRegime==="RANGING_CHOP")marketRegimeScore=10
}
else{
if(currentRegime==="TRENDING_BEAR")marketRegimeScore=20;
else if(currentRegime==="RANGING_CHOP")marketRegimeScore=10
}
const lastBodySize=Math.abs(lastBar.close-lastBar.open)
  || currentPrice*1e-4;
let sweepCompleted=false;
let sweepRejectionScore=1.5;
if(isBuy){
const bottomWick=Math.min(lastBar.open,lastBar.close)-lastBar.low;
if(bottomWick>lastBodySize*1.3){
sweepCompleted=true;
sweepRejectionScore=9
}
else if(bottomWick>lastBodySize*.8){
sweepRejectionScore=5.5
}

}
else{
const topWick=lastBar.high-Math.max(lastBar.open,lastBar.close);
if(topWick>lastBodySize*1.3){
sweepCompleted=true;
sweepRejectionScore=9
}
else if(topWick>lastBodySize*.8){
sweepRejectionScore=5.5
}

}
const fakeBreakoutSweep=lastBar.high>=highestHighLast10*.999
  || lastBar.low<=lowestLowLast10*1.001?6:3;
const liquiditySweepScore=Number((sweepRejectionScore+fakeBreakoutSweep).toFixed(2));
const priceVsEmaScore=isBuy?currentPrice>ema200Val?5:1:currentPrice<ema200Val?5:1;
let trendSeqScore=1.5;
if(isBuy){
if(lastBar.high>prevBar.high
  && lastBar.low>prevBar.low)trendSeqScore=7;
else if(lastBar.high>prevBar.high
  || lastBar.low>prevBar.low)trendSeqScore=4
}
else{
if(lastBar.low<prevBar.low
  && lastBar.high<prevBar.high)trendSeqScore=7;
else if(lastBar.low<prevBar.low
  || lastBar.high<prevBar.high)trendSeqScore=4
}
const trendStrengthScore=Math.abs(smaFast-smaSlow)/currentPrice>.0015?3:1;
const trendConfluenceScore=Number((priceVsEmaScore+trendSeqScore+trendStrengthScore).toFixed(2));

hardBlockerTriggered=true;
blockReasonDetail+="Market regime mismatch;
 "

  && (symbol.startsWith("XAU")
  || symbol.startsWith("BTC"))){

blockReasonDetail+="Opposing higher-timeframe structure;

let finalScore=Math.min(100,Math.max(0,Math.round(totalScoreRaw)));
if(hardBlockerTriggered){
finalScore=Math.min(finalScore,48)
}
const outStructure=Math.round(marketStructureScore*(20/25));
const outPriceAction=Math.round(trendConfluenceScore*(20/15));
const outMomentum=Math.round(liquiditySweepScore*(20/15));
const outVolume=Math.round(htfAlignmentScore*(20/25));
const outVolatility=Math.round(marketRegimeScore*(20/20));
const detailsText=hardBlockerTriggered?`\u274C BLOCKED SETUP: Blocker rules triggered: ${

/25%], Market Regime Detection [${

/20%], Liquidity Sweep Confluence [${

}
/15%], Trend Confluence [${
trendConfluenceScore
}
/15%].`;

score:finalScore,breakdown:{
structure:outStructure,priceAction:outPriceAction,momentum:outMomentum,volume:outVolume,volatility:outVolatility
}
,details:detailsText
